<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>Ai learning</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {

}

	body {
		margin: 2em auto;

body {

}

a,

}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;

}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;

}

	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;

	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {

}

.indented {

}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;

}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {

}

	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {

}

}

table,
th,
td {

}

table {

}

th,

}

}

ol,
ul {

ul > li {

}

.to-do-children-checked {

}

ul > li {

	padding-inline-start: 1.6em;
}

.mono ol > li {

.toggle {
	padding-inline-start: 0em;

.toggle > li > details {
	padding-left: 1.7em;
}

.toggle > li > details > summary {
	margin-left: -1.1em;

.collection-title {

time {

}

}

p > .user {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

td > .user,
td > time {
	white-space: nowrap;
}

input[type="checkbox"] {
	transform: scale(1.5);
	margin-right: 0.6em;
	vertical-align: middle;
}

p {
	margin-top: 0.5em;
	margin-bottom: 0.5em;
}

.image {
	border: none;

}

.code,
code {

	border-radius: 3px;

}

code {
	color: #eb5757;
}

.code {
	padding: 1.5em 1em;
}

.code-wrap {
	white-space: pre-wrap;

}

.code > code {
	background: none;
	padding: 0;
	font-size: 100%;

}

blockquote {
	font-size: 1em;
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding-left: 1em;
	border-left: 3px solid rgb(55, 53, 47);
}

blockquote.quote-large {
	font-size: 1.25em;
}

.bookmark {
	text-decoration: none;
	max-height: 8em;
	padding: 0;
	display: flex;
	width: 100%;
	align-items: stretch;
}

.bookmark-title {
	font-size: 0.85em;
	overflow: hidden;
	text-overflow: ellipsis;
	height: 1.75em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.bookmark-text {
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
}

.bookmark-info {
	flex: 4 1 180px;
	padding: 12px 14px 14px;
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
	justify-content: space-between;
}

.bookmark-image {
	width: 33%;
	flex: 1 1 180px;
	display: block;
	position: relative;
	object-fit: cover;
	border-radius: 1px;
}

.bookmark-description {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
	font-size: 0.75em;
	overflow: hidden;
	max-height: 4.5em;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.bookmark-href {
	font-size: 0.75em;
	margin-top: 0.25em;
}

</style></head><body><article id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-bfc4-d3d3aef114fb" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Ai learning</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-bd27-f7988e5eac2c" class="">Good.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-b2b4-df3e991f5d11" class="">Here are <strong>100 more advanced manipulation &amp; cunning invariants</strong>, deeper layer — focusing on micro-psychology, network dynamics, time-horizon exploitation, and structural asymmetries.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-b3ab-fa356cc6120a" class="">No repetition from prior lists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-a06c-de2edfd0684f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-8a6e-fef02a403570" class="">100 Additional Advanced Manipulation Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-b749-e21fcf43c709"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-845d-c46b97dc538f" class="">1. Emotional Anticipation Trap</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-95e3-cef38a5a412d" class="">Pre-activate expectation before delivering stimulus.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-ad97-dd481cbbd6cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Anticipation \uparrow \Rightarrow DopamineBaseline \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-aff0-d41c7cf3ee14"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-93fc-fada042cfccf" class="">2. Narrative Pre-Seeding</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-804c-d3ba6fcaea7e" class="">Plant interpretation before event occurs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-9aba-c1aba3277240" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Perception_{event} = f(PreFrame)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-ba52-e104c0e2da62"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-8ab4-e380ec27ceeb" class="">3. Status Reversal Shock</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-913b-f9431715de58" class="">Temporarily lower your own status to disarm.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-a8a9-e818cfd012f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Defense \downarrow \text{ when } DominanceSignal \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-a634-e0afbf247804"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-ba06-f04ad91e6f4a" class="">4. Conditional Scarcity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-b083-d8bdc23455e3" class="">Scarcity tied to identity increases compliance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-a697-d7943947fba4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto Scarcity \times IdentityRelevance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-8075-f846db945e3e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-8bd3-f8f89d014c66" class="">5. Emotional Echo Extension</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-b864-da72c5d3fb35" class="">Repeat emotional phrasing to extend intensity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-9a83-d76f3aff99e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EmotionPersistence \propto LinguisticMirroring</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-add7-d61b61a6ca51"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-bca6-e3e462b0edab" class="">6. Soft Entry Law</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-8ab8-c676cacb00f2" class="">Influence increases when initial demand is invisible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-9e08-c0126b4bea39" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DetectionRisk \propto EntryIntensity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-988d-e4a63874929b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-9394-e6613a8b1213" class="">7. Micro-Validation Hook</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-a632-c944df25ee42" class="">Frequent subtle approval creates attachment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-869d-e020d96832d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto ValidationFrequency_{micro}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-9829-eece653946ab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-b292-c8676bfb74e6" class="">8. Self-Disclosure Leverage</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-8116-decdf5888d55" class="">Encourage target confession first.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-bdca-f3bcac85de7d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Power \propto Asymmetry_{Disclosure}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-8f22-c9e9b990b209"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-a61f-cd3b9b46f138" class="">9. Delayed Reward Frustration</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-aa6c-c67a9fcf270e" class="">Delay reward to increase fixation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-8da2-cf14c3d9821b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fixation \propto RewardDelay</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-9487-f2c790b90bb1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-8a0c-cf7c23931f6d" class="">10. Reversal of Accusation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-bfa5-c7a094dc09bf" class="">Accuse before being accused.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-b0ae-c4338519da9d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Credibility \propto FirstNarrative</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-8018-df0b3e71dfcf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-97f7-ef0b65a4ad89" class="">11. Ambiguous Generosity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-ad4f-eaa0f8c30a37" class="">Offer unclear benefit to create dependency.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-afc6-c4df586ad248" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dependency \propto UnclearObligation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-a43e-d4780cdac4a4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-83cd-c9b991f85a53" class="">12. Emotional Contrast Principle</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-9136-c7ba828e9e25" class="">Follow negative with positive to amplify effect.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-875d-f1339226c7f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Impact \propto EmotionalContrast</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-9298-d9f8b9172dd6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-876a-e04ad9d481b9" class="">13. Symbolic Ownership</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-aa88-d8ef7d6042b5" class="">Create perception of shared ownership.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-a7c3-d6d32fb91fcc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Commitment \propto PerceivedJointInvestment</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9bc6-e1a09b4db5b5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a3e5-d2b90827e5b1" class="">14. Controlled Vulnerability Display</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-8db2-e05c42ae84a3" class="">Show selective weakness to increase trust.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-a72e-c1c01b326176" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trust \propto PerceivedAuthenticity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-92e2-d10b5ab55f8d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-85ed-c2db5d962da3" class="">15. Slow Normalization Drift</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-a79f-e5383c289295" class="">Gradual drift in standards reduces detection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-8763-fd7c2aedd4bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Tolerance(t) = T_0 + \epsilon t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-8aec-f6ccda4b798a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-8d6a-ca8274f706dc" class="">16. Language Reframing Shift</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-9f91-ef8a7ca7fad0" class="">Replace harmful words with neutral framing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-a935-e493359644a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Acceptance \propto SemanticSoftening</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-a6ba-ed39e1108ff0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-964c-c5f7a5780548" class="">17. Cognitive Overconfidence Exploit</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-9b43-fa8b16ad9ba2" class="">Targets with high confidence often under-check.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-8f16-f0e92e00b668" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Detection \propto 1 / Overconfidence</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-b920-ddc744c05db8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-a65f-ffe683dac009" class="">18. Reward Prediction Error Cycling</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-97a3-c60c8703ed1f" class="">Alternate reward magnitude unpredictably.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-838d-ca38684ca771" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Addiction \propto RewardVariance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-be83-e24b8d23afe2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-a83f-c50d1fd3d345" class="">19. Emotional Replacement</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-91a3-c5f1a1a6dc54" class="">Replace one emotion with another before resolution.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-be0a-d36516551676" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Clarity \propto EmotionalCompletion</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-a09b-e51148cc71f8" class="">Disrupt completion → confusion persists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-988c-c1a1a6ba18d5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-bcfd-e6d146d25f03" class="">20. Self-Justification Amplification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-afcf-ee2f90b6636f" class="">After minor compliance, encourage public commitment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-a176-c333ef90e20e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ExitProbability \propto 1 / PublicCommitment</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-9d5c-e6693b71a70a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-bd4e-f9301a341a86" class="">21. Perceived Rarity of Insight</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-a14e-dcfe4f331052" class="">Claim unique understanding.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-881f-f311738cfe2f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trust \propto PerceivedExclusivity_{Insight}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-847f-c5f7d0f29eaa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-8f5f-edeaf7d9596f" class="">22. Controlled Chaos Strategy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-8e46-c5741a1ec6de" class="">Introduce instability, then offer structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-9f85-dcf367f188d8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Authority \propto ChaosControl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-8c22-d06043abb0ff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-befa-fdf92caded32" class="">23. Subtle Boundary Violation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-846f-e708e084509e" class="">Tiny oversteps test tolerance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-ade1-dd756f670704" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FutureCompliance \propto BoundaryElasticity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-9534-d95f146557c4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-b62b-d3ad3a0ae4c2" class="">24. Emotional Monopolization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-bbd4-f116a782aa6a" class="">Become primary emotional regulator.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-a26c-f162d26f7295" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dependency \propto EmotionalMonopoly</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-a7cc-f28e39ff3174"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-8b88-fd6f10f09385" class="">25. Gradual Identity Shift</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-876c-d73b607b3986" class="">Encourage small identity redefinitions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-9bda-e2bf640259d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Identity(t+1) = Identity(t) + \delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-92f4-c34c1d0d75ba"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-b606-d48c2482c489" class="">26. Guilt–Redemption Loop</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-9656-f80310bda6a2" class="">Induce guilt, then offer redemption.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-8d25-d296b1027be9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Bond \propto GuiltReliefCycle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-963a-e8680a1e7018"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-8828-d5b6902b38d2" class="">27. Resource Withholding</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-abaa-e7280c28c8ef" class="">Withhold attention to increase pursuit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-a732-c235c597ddce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Desire \propto IntermittentAvailability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-8ded-ffead17d623e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-9f79-c9b023ec9dab" class="">28. Ambiguous Future Projection</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-874e-f4f73f18108d" class="">Keep future undefined but positive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-953d-d6f5fceb768f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto FutureAmbiguity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-a564-de886ed1989b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-aaba-d088a879f03b" class="">29. Authority Inflation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-b141-f58f81a3175e" class="">Exaggerate expertise subtly.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-8ec8-c298699decb7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto PerceivedCompetence</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-9829-f228becbc3e7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-aa12-f692aa095fc3" class="">30. Information Flood</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-a72a-fa1ff5a7dd56" class="">Overwhelm with excess detail.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-8b1c-d89531c3372f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Clarity \propto 1 / InfoDensity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-8e07-f719e97b7f62"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-94c4-d1ae0f48e09f" class="">31. Emotional Borrowing</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-b426-fdc04623bbb6" class="">Attach message to existing fear/desire.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-acb3-fc34e028501e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto EmotionalRelevance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-a8b5-f32eb9ceda37"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-a388-ca76029cf9f9" class="">32. Context Switching</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-bf2d-e4958b2d2839" class="">Shift discussion to destabilize opponent.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-b535-d140276ee860" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CognitiveLoad \uparrow \Rightarrow Resistance \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-be92-c066df3d783a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-8c1e-e6c30f3cd197" class="">33. Insecurity Amplification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-bbbc-f00c639f1f76" class="">Subtly highlight deficiency.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-b317-ecb21fb49533" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto InsecurityLevel</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9be7-ebd55b148604"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-b616-d0503f95aabd" class="">34. Role Assignment</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-8817-fb09d2bb6e4b" class="">Assign identity label.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-a61e-f80e0366190f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Behavior \propto AssignedRole</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-93c6-d4cecf2424f8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9ce9-f61d4eb84666" class="">35. Overexposure Desensitization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-9431-e88daed9455f" class="">Repeat stimulus until normalized.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-af66-c09d5c84e37f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance \propto 1 / Exposure</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-adca-c103e9adc37e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-86ca-c7a89ecd54ee" class="">36. Strategic Delay</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-812f-c63f1bbbd2b0" class="">Delay response to increase anxiety.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-92f7-ff88324f0dd9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

Calibration Invariant</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-b798-c0d227bb4a89" class="">Manipulation succeeds only if stimulus amplitude &lt; detection threshold.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-bfbc-f596f56e964a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

Emotional Matching Law</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-bff5-d9782e4c15f2" class="">Influence increases when emotional tone matches target state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-8b9a-ea5562163f7c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trust \propto EmotionalResonance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-810e-c92048ceccaa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-af13-f27ead5014da" class="">3.
