Cognitive Fatigue Window</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-a87f-db505b51c023" class="">Decision quality collapses under fatigue.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-b43b-ce24bb817073" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Rationality \propto 1 / Fatigue</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-9b79-d3e768bc19ea"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-bcf4-d5674a427354" class="">11. Future Promise Discounting</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-888a-f53f73766092" class="">Future reward reduces present resistance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-9ab5-ed9d5ff0bf15" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto PromisedFutureValue</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-b933-fa0a35cae310"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-889e-f463391daeb5" class="">12. Fear–Hope Oscillation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-821b-c9b1dff7fa38" class="">Alternating fear and hope increases emotional control.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-81ea-f901f4356dd0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto Fear \times Relief</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-96e6-c04cef523da6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-85b7-dc10e7ef0c09" class="">13. Exclusivity Effect</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-92c1-d87f0bbe7a08" class="">Exclusive access increases attachment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-b1b0-cebb683a773d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Bond \propto PerceivedExclusivity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-96a1-e67eed62fc4f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-8894-f1740a830bc6" class="">14. Confusion Strategy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-9496-cd9c41de56a2" class="">Confusion reduces analytical defense.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-a569-ff9664c0891f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Defense \propto 1 / Ambiguity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-9f81-f6c3f26e20dc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-a7b1-d11c5ee9391d" class="">15. Guilt Induction Mechanism</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-a540-cf526446cbf1" class="">Guilt lowers boundary enforcement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-ace9-e6a5768638b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BoundaryStrength \propto 1 / Guilt</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-a14f-f63169f2b1a6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-9646-f453fb848a7f" class="">16. Moral Superiority Framing</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-99b6-da1fc3e9a28b" class="">Claim moral high ground to silence opposition.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-b880-c3203156f1a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
OppositionCost \propto MoralFrameStrength</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-b703-c7456a6451bf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-b00d-c099d5fc7077" class="">17. Flattery Anchoring</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-8fbb-e81e467fb4b6" class="">Early validation increases openness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-86bd-cafc02aa4567" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Openness \propto InitialPraise</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-b15d-d30bd5ca450f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-860b-d206f5454abc" class="">18. Social Isolation Acceleration</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-9036-ceb9698a9cc4" class="">Remove dissenting voices to amplify influence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-bccc-ebd91926b5dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto 1 / ExternalFeedback</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-aca2-ee19af05ad5a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-bde0-eb6769587861" class="">19. Threat Normalization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-a8d6-ca737004df44" class="">Gradually normalize unacceptable behavior.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-864c-c7fdb9370015" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Tolerance(t) = T_0 + \epsilon t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-9e73-e5bdfc6d7d00"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-8650-d6925297a78a" class="">20. Micro-Commitment Ladder</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-a1f8-dc12f239ddde" class="">Small commitments escalate over time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-b86e-fc47d5beed2a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Commitment_{n+1} &gt; Commitment_n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-af43-eebb89e586a5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-8a09-ec98acc24b72" class="">21. Emotional Synchronization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-beea-e574651e993e" class="">Mirroring increases trust.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-9598-c52ec34348c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trust \propto BehavioralMatching</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-bd07-d3484f9b64d7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-bdc1-e89f61431433" class="">22. Scarcity Illusion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-8953-db70e680e9ba" class="">Artificial scarcity amplifies desire.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-aeae-d0a84b1d8510" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Desire \propto 1 / PerceivedAvailability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-9716-fc29e02a55c4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-89ad-f8fb91bd002c" class="">23. Social Comparison Trigger</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-81b7-ff2fe56619b4" class="">Comparison increases insecurity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-bf65-e9bc42b57771" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Insecurity \propto UpwardComparison</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-8f57-c6600a4dc702"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9c8e-e9c8d0c85569" class="">24. Shock Induction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-a9b6-cc1839bc0c84" class="">Sudden emotional spikes disrupt reasoning.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-aa48-ef334e293919" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Reasoning \propto 1 / EmotionalShock</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-9277-f50010520b44"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-b842-ff9b78c3d66c" class="">25. Reciprocity Overload</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-8870-e6d5a69e4c8d" class="">Excess giving creates obligation imbalance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-881c-da23b5aa1887" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Obligation \propto UnsolicitedBenefit</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9db9-cc410df47655"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-94f7-cc349df15fb7" class="">26. Emotional Dependency Curve</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-a9fc-da93b213c606" class="">Dependency rises when autonomy falls.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-a447-e2471d8b41c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dependency \propto 1 / Autonomy</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-978e-ccade613d05f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-bab9-e454a81486bd" class="">27. Secret Sharing Bond</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-9ff7-cdabca4dca78" class="">Shared secrets increase loyalty.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-a821-e6af4885efca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Bond \propto ConfidentialDisclosure</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-a0ab-e016e41a68c7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-a773-caa85175c107" class="">28. Victim Role Exploitation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-970b-f666c01b052d" class="">Claim victimhood to deflect accountability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-bd76-d041e4dfe41a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Accountability \propto 1 / VictimNarrative</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-a707-dc98bd724b37"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-b8b3-c213fe1af02c" class="">29. Triangulated Jealousy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-b2cd-ed04769db725" class="">Introduce third party to increase competition.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-a64f-fbd8a14b75cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto RivalPresence</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-ac8c-f32b4405530a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-b750-d8c8884002d5" class="">30. Time Scarcity Compression</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-98d7-fa2b39d1d335" class="">Reduce thinking time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-a8a4-c31659830098" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto 1 / DecisionTime</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-998a-dd34f8d1244e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-9d7d-c820a7ee982d" class="">31. Emotional Volatility Trap</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-979a-fb9e3cb3b32c" class="">Unpredictability increases attention.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-982d-f55d99e4d81b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attention \propto RewardVariance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-b0e6-d8c64f1237e1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-a34d-def8ce092828" class="">32. Dependency Reinforcement</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-b23e-f101bfd52398" class="">Alternate support and withdrawal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-8ab3-d12865327feb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Bond \propto Variability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-8a4d-f1543e3f8eb6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-acb7-f24aeac4fa2e" class="">33. Identity Fusion Lock</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-8ada-ff976b175875" class="">Fuse belief with identity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-8954-e3779829af6f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance \propto IdentityStability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-a79a-fcb63a378f06"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-bab1-f7b132e53d08" class="">34. Echo Chamber Amplification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-a4e0-c84d39e5aaab" class="">Repeat beliefs in closed group.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-b6b2-f78f7ca8b788" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BeliefStrength \propto ClosedLoopReinforcement</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-92ef-c7453c797b6c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-9bde-db77e7ff34e4" class="">35. Authority Scarcity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-86f0-ef0a095c0b6e" class="">Rare authority figures amplify obedience.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-bbfe-cb9d025d0c72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Obedience \propto AuthorityRarity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-8e6b-d5a0855f17df"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-a14a-d7966c9cec04" class="">36. Emotional Contagion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-a5e9-cfc732a4aa6d" class="">Group emotion spreads rapidly.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-a267-f6f366645c44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EmotionSpread \propto Density \times Intensity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-bdd7-c4f7c2e75b3b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-9a85-d9040dcd4db6" class="">37. Status Threat Activation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-8e97-eb76b0a6605b" class="">Threaten status to increase compliance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-8eea-cad192fc8266" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto StatusThreat</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-a5fe-d355944c0b98"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-b9cc-f5365f451d4b" class="">38. Conditional Approval</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-9499-e09bff31b3f4" class="">Approval tied to behavior shapes compliance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-884b-fb232a7c4e44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BehaviorShift \propto ConditionalAcceptance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-8be1-fde3a5d65662"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-9e01-c0091932a384" class="">39. Shame Amplification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-ac54-e70ff547f4e0" class="">Shame reduces resistance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-bd8e-ffe26d25de03" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Boundary \propto 1 / Shame</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-8ddc-ff07038d412d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-b597-e0870393607e" class="">40. Projection Strategy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-9d3a-fe7ef6a8110b" class="">Accuse others of own behavior.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-9daa-e454d6d66555" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Confusion \propto Projection</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-8c88-d68c7e3e2f19"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-9acc-c935a6857994" class="">41. Gradual Escalation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-a628-d23778f91999" class="">Slow increase avoids detection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-bc82-fa85ab576b06" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DetectionRisk \propto EscalationSpeed</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-9a78-e61e6860dd80"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-9984-e0d1e1a36e92" class="">42. Resource Gatekeeping</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-b7c1-e34181d64a43" class="">Control access to resources.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-9fc1-fe8ff8af036c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Power \propto ResourceControl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-b399-c8f74461cd09"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-9226-f3f73b1eb48d" class="">43. Emotional Dependency Lock</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-aeec-e718eec961bd" class="">Repeated validation cycles.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-9593-d25435bbfb5e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dependency \propto ValidationFrequency</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-85b3-d90ebe56983e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-a425-fc9e750bf7a3" class="">44. Moral Reframing</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-bece-e16102c3618a" class="">Redefine harmful action as virtuous.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-823e-f4b9c1bbfd95" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Acceptance \propto FrameShift</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-8166-d66472255735"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-b45b-e6d7b091950c" class="">45. Isolation–Dependency Coupling</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-815f-f6c4c61eb0e7" class="">Isolation increases attachment intensity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-9996-c69e10ed5018" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto Isolation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-ae89-f48da6a9c4b3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-849d-c3d4112cdd85" class="">46. Blame Transfer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-aa89-d361971123a7" class="">Shift responsibility outward.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-b2c8-e9bd695a48fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SelfAccountability \propto 1 / BlameShift</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-8d78-e8f8ef9da805"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-8ad2-c4f4abbac531" class="">47. Strategic Silence</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-8b82-d53814d94a0c" class="">Silence increases projection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-a7b0-dac5eb2dd4d4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Projection \propto SilenceDuration</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9c76-eee0186bc973"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-b9d2-cf8df41ca8a7" class="">48. Emotional Exhaustion Strategy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-a203-e540dea2f984" class="">Exhaust target until compliance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-aee7-f1fddd7b560d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance \propto EnergyReserve</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-a96c-fcb1afc641d2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-aa65-cd5d01b45576" class="">49. Inconsistency Destabilization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-ac5f-d9d61d90dd0c" class="">Contradictions create confusion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-b5b8-ec783694b634" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Clarity \propto 1 / Inconsistency</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-90d0-c4e3a1c91967"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-83cc-dc19f16740df" class="">50. Fluctuating Affection</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-8746-fd63bab27c52" class="">Creates insecurity bond.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-a05e-de61284d4ff8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto AffectionVariance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-852b-ddb5db32c0d0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p confusion</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-b9da-ec09faeb447f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Fine inflation → behavioral expansion</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-a364-f7d8490f2f10" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Data asymmetry → power imbalance</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-aa8c-fbf887762dff" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Narrative distraction → issue avoidance</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-aee3-e1c98b8242a3" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Selective transparency → perception management</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-a74b-ea876597c56e" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Controlled opposition → illusion of choice</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-9413-e2125cfd56b3" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Incentive distortion → behavior shaping</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-b7b8-d33bef182b95"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-8c50-e7f7ac3895b4" class="">91–100: Meta-Level Invariants</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-a3e9-f451bb9bfd1a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Instability amplifies manipulability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-9f3c-e01068460af7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Fatigue lowers detection</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-babe-fb16c1b2d988" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Identity insecurity multiplies compliance</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-b6f4-d52a31529f27" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Group polarization reduces nuance</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-ab5f-eb322e3ba42f" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Narrative simplicity beats complexity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-af27-dfcb1ec70c69" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Emotion outruns logic</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-9912-f3a31b9d7a95" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Scarcity beats abundance in urgency</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-a469-cf16e458ec52" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Silence can control more than speech</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-8468-e8d188dbe54e" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Trust erosion increases susceptibility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-948e-ff016c1c79c2" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Stable nervous systems are hardest to manipulate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-894a-cb5ff5c75314"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-a618-c71f496f5fbc" class="">If you want, next level can be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-8f46-c067305b39b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">100 Anti-Manipulation Counter-Invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-9d91-c7b984cb8f7b" class="">• Ultra-Deep Seduction vs Manipulation Differentiation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-922b-e45dd1de71ef" class="">• AI-powered psychological targeting model</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-afff-cbabd1c28c40" class="">• Or a Master Unified Manipulation Equation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-ace7-fb35aca3e4b0" class="">Choose.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-abb3-f932c9cf14d4" class="">Good.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-8320-fc5247431192" class="">Now we go to the next layer:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-acef-c5da0c95bfcf" class="">SYSTEMIC MANIPULATION DYNAMICS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-b3cb-f5635bbc5a5e" class="">(Game Theory + Multi-Agent + AI Amplification + Long Horizon Survival)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-b255-df7ad7923b8a" class="">We move from individual manipulation to <strong>network-scale strategic influence systems</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-bc2e-ecb24145bde9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-816d-d09e60dd0c5a" class="">I. Multi-Agent Manipulation Game Model</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-8423-f0f8f75ff71d" class="">Assume a population of N agents.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-9ca5-c885538e0663" class="">Each agent has:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-981e-ed90d38b5c2c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Awareness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-b111-ee27a1f71eec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-b86d-d8dc4e0fa2ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Vulnerability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-a003-cb0223753a2f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Social connectivity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-ac20-e8de82bc311d" class="">Manipulator chooses target set .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-b90f-d0c45312c697" class="">Total influence yield:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-adf5-c65683fc27d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
TotalGain = \sum_{i \in T} \frac{V_i \cdot C_i}{A_i \cdot R_i}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-b4bb-ec13189617e5" class="">Key insight:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-953a-c754c9aa1291" class="">You do not need to control everyone.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-8fb7-e545e5ab5440" class="">You control high-centrality nodes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-82ad-f427e6f477df"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-92a7-f04d29938f46" class="">1. Network Cascade Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-8d66-f600a75d031b" class="">If high-connectivity nodes are influenced:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9605-c73abcae0063" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CascadeProbability \propto Centrality^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-8dd0-d8bc42267659" class="">Influence spreads nonlinearly.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-a985-ef4f9a428653" class="">This is why:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-8577-e03ecf4dd8ac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Influencers matter more than masses.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-a304-e1be3c72a286" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Media nodes matter more than individuals.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-9229-e4dd5c55e051"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-9193-db942e2de668" class="">II. Manipulation as Evolutionary Strategy</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-b967-c519469d096e" class="">Across generations:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-b949-c6165ef99be3" class="">Strategies that maximize:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-9c8b-fba114a5e436" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fitness = ResourceAccess + ReproductiveSuccess + SurvivalProbability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-9170-cfc1d5cf1a3f" class="">will propagate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-8dbf-fb4eabe9eae9" class="">Manipulative strategies survive if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-b635-c9cbbc381e36" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gain &gt; SocialPenalty</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-9c64-f44a37c49122" class="">If detection cost is low → manipulation proliferates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9dfa-dd47b922966a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-823d-f4bcbf13e3ad" class="">III. Long-Term Stability Curve of Manipulators</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-b4e4-e30a73f72531" class="">Short-term:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-a11a-c0ab446fe61b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HighManipulation \Rightarrow HighGain</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-af43-ff82d49f6944" class="">Long-term:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-983c-d433c21a4530" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HighManipulation \Rightarrow TrustErosion \Rightarrow SystemCollapse</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-bd79-f19056b5ba9a" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-b874-fc0ef9d172ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Sustainability \propto Trust</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-b5bc-eaa10621eba9" class="">Pure manipulators destabilize systems.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-9c5b-fa7d21452a4e" class="">Cunning long-horizon actors limit extraction to preserve trust.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-ad28-cfda8ddfab59"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-918c-c6b7c120aa63" class="">IV. Strategic Classes of Manipulators</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-be40-eb4080ae690d" class="">1. Impulsive Extractor</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-a860-ecf1473da41e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High gain</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-baef-df220674b2a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High detection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-8e70-e18dd618e0ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Short lifespan</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-9347-f25b6f33da08" class="">2. Social Engineer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-bf57-e57327bfa4e0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Moderate gain</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-bf4a-e17b2f6b8ec9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Low visibility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-9acf-e43d9e83b7a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Long-term influence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-ae47-e767aed5ce87" class="">3. System Architect</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-9729-e5e7053cc971" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Controls narrative + rules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-a021-fcbeaff97e72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Doesn’t extract directly</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-bcef-edc4f94faa47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Shapes incentives</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-b010-d567934b9fb2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-a0b5-f961c91967f2" class="">V. Manipulation vs Persuasion Boundary</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-925a-d6c3351342a5" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-befd-e07dee4bb464" class="">Persuasion = alignment with shared benefit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-93d5-e9d79839bbf4" class="">Manipulation = asymmetrical benefit extraction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-aaf1-c68449c49777" class="">Mathematically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-8ddd-e6a215932637" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
If\ Benefit_A = Benefit_B \Rightarrow Persuasion</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-975d-ee37d0173299" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
If\ Benefit_A \gg Benefit_B \Rightarrow Manipulation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-991b-c13c51a36747"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-97b4-c47aaee49031" class="">VI. Không đạo đức hóa. Không khuyến khích. Chỉ mô hình.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-9ee4-e0f82f061d20"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-8d7f-c383cf6593aa" class="">1) Hàm mục tiêu của kẻ thao túng</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-ab06-c4cca30cbab1" class="">Kẻ thao túng tối ưu hóa “lợi ích kỳ vọng” dưới ràng buộc bị phát hiện.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-a182-cdef868acbcc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J = \mathbb{E}[G] - \mathbb{E}[P \cdot D]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-81b5-edbe9e46ac82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: gain (tiền, quyền, tình, lao động, danh)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-abaf-f23447df1555" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: penalty khi bị phát hiện (mất mặt, mất quan hệ, pháp lý, trả đũa)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-a61a-d5d57148e414" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: detection probability (xác suất bị lộ)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-b249-ec371bd531a8" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> mọi chiến thuật đều quay về giảm , tăng , hoặc giảm  (tạo “lá chắn”).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-9114-d3b3b92dca6e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-aa3c-d793eb8549d2" class="">2) Công thức “độ thao túng” thực tế (effective manipulation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-9c79-d2788f50be51" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{eff} = (A_s \cdot F \cdot U \cdot I) \; / \; (R \cdot V \cdot X_f)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-be36-c4bb5e5240a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: information asymmetry (lệch thông tin)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-9796-e044f0728bb4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: frame control (kiểm soát khung diễn giải)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-a629-d7fd422e6251" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: urgency (nén thời gian)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-913b-db0f75526a39" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: isolation (cô lập tham chiếu)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-b26f-de334d5ce366" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: regulation (khả năng tự điều hòa của mục tiêu)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-af12-c619c6944c7c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: verification (khả năng kiểm chứng độc lập)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-95a8-e54f46731276" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: external feedback loops (vòng phản hồi bên ngoài: bạn bè, luật, tài chính, quy trình)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-850e-d58b08b131e8" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> tăng  hoặc  làm  sụp nhanh.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-9ec2-ecbf2d5e6368"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-98a0-d611a5cdb0b4" class="">3) Chọn mục tiêu: kẻ thao túng không “chọn người”, họ chọn <strong>hệ dễ khai thác</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-895a-d10049aac6dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
TargetScore = \frac{Need \times Openness \times Instability}{Boundaries \times Verification \times Support}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-aa94-fe584ae4c231" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Need”: nhu cầu (tình cảm, công nhận, tài chính, thuộc về)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-af43-e939d51d8e43" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Openness”: sẵn lòng tin/cho cơ hội</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-9ecc-e268d213c44d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Instability”: tải thần kinh/đời sống bất ổn</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-83f1-f9d91f81a6cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Boundaries”: ranh giới rõ</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-9a18-d338c1796a16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Verification”: kiểm chứng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-8c6e-c4bccb8daa3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Support”: mạng hỗ trợ</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-bccd-f242b37bf8b1" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> người càng thiếu “support + verification”, càng bị chọn.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-933a-fcaf1c443e0a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-b0d6-ea8275872476" class="">4) Luật “đòn bẩy tối đa” (max leverage)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-9bb2-c4de56b07c15" class="">Đòn bẩy cao nhất nằm ở 3 biến: <strong>identity, belonging, status</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-8675-ef14886fcf2a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \uparrow \text{ mạnh nhất khi } (IdentityThreat \uparrow) \lor (BelongingThreat \uparrow) \lor (StatusThreat \uparrow)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-bb09-c3f44aaaac5e" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> mọi cú thao túng lớn đều gắn với “bạn là ai / bạn thuộc về đâu / bạn có giá trị gì”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-b7f3-daedd9f14de1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-9466-e3551f6dac9c" class="">5) Vòng lặp nghiện: biến thiên thưởng-phạt</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-be7d-f9d22a3e4151" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Bond \propto Var(Reward) \times Uncertainty</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-8a48-e9aa1457f163" class="">Và:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-b6a8-fb5cd740bd3c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attention \propto PredictionError</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-b939-e63f5e9b51fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tạo sai số dự đoán (mập mờ, lúc nóng lúc lạnh) → mục tiêu tăng chú ý để “giải mã”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-95a0-c179079544b2" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> ổn định tạo yên; biến thiên tạo ám ảnh.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-911e-f7c9409312fe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-8ef1-d1a8ab8a0ccf" class="">6) Gaslighting là “tấn công vào self-trust”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-b176-da5431a8790b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SelfTrust_{t+1} = SelfTrust_t - \delta \cdot RealityDistortion</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-b9f9-cad610bc8de4" class="">Khi :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8166-f9b523b98bd0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dependence \uparrow \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-9859-e95e9ff666f1" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> gaslighting thành công khi mục tiêu mất chuẩn nội tại và phải mượn chuẩn của người thao túng.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-aea6-ef5197c5e07b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-8dd2-dee1c4822ae7" class="">7) Triangulation: tăng quyền lực bằng bên thứ ba</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-a995-c06ddbdac3cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
