Power = DirectControl + \rho \cdot ThirdPartyPressure</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-b887-f3c501928eb7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bên thứ ba có thể là người cũ, “cộng đồng”, “chuyên gia”, “gia đình”, “sếp”, “đạo đức”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-b806-c814cf8fe320" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> càng nhiều “tham chiếu ngoài” mà mục tiêu tôn trọng, càng dễ bị ép.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-a744-df7dcc0e0203"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9ff2-f1a4bfef3339" class="">8) “Plausible deniability” là tài sản cốt lõi của cunning</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a245-f4230c7a30d4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D \downarrow \text{ khi } Ambiguity \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-836b-f24f57075997" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Survival \propto NarrativeFlexibility</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-8a5b-e929d0e5a398" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> càng có thể diễn giải lại, càng khó kết tội.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-9c4a-f64fce732a51"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-bea2-c96561459491" class="">9) Kỹ thuật “moral laundering” (rửa động cơ bằng đạo đức)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-945d-d4c71cfe2402" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Extraction \rightarrow FramedAs(\text{Care / Duty / Destiny / Logic})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-8ba0-ea00f5a62f2b" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> khi lấy lợi ích, họ đóng gói bằng “ý nghĩa cao hơn” để mục tiêu tự nguyện.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-a05a-f8fe20aa01ec"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-9d13-ef24e75e0117" class="">10) “Foot-in-the-door” và “sunk cost”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-a3c4-caea0775d48b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FutureCompliance \propto PriorInvestment</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-953c-db15cdf6553b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ExitCost_{psych} \uparrow \text{ theo } Investment</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-ab46-fe1f737a57eb" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> càng đầu tư, càng khó rút dù biết sai.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-81b7-e4da85080074"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-9695-e27ea45ac6e5" class="">11) Luật “cạn vùng đệm” (depletion)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-8cbf-d274a139810b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance \propto EnergyReserve</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9184-c2290ad82294" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EnergyReserve \downarrow \Rightarrow Boundary \downarrow \Rightarrow Compliance \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-9128-c41ebdea7b99" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> mệt, đói, thiếu ngủ, cô đơn → tăng xác suất bị thao túng, không phải vì ngu mà vì sinh lý.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-8502-e09836cb27c7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-83de-ccd5e08de066" class="">12) Manipulation theo chuỗi (multi-stage pipeline)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-9157-cb3e940a22ab" class=""><strong>Stage 1: Hook</strong> (kích hoạt nhu cầu)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-9327-e60ee4993f12" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Hook = NeedActivation + Validation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-a81f-eed1f99bf062" class=""><strong>Stage 2: Narrow</strong> (nén lựa chọn)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-8839-c2a1632b642f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ChoiceSet \downarrow \Rightarrow Compliance \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-95ec-f905df5b17de" class=""><strong>Stage 3: Bind</strong> (tạo ràng buộc)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-9dba-cb958bc95176" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Bind = Investment + Uncertainty + Isolation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-9fd5-d2053f39fe41" class=""><strong>Stage 4: Extract</strong> (lấy lợi ích)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-add5-dfd460580379" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Extract = Access \times Leverage</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-939d-fab8f2a1c0de" class=""><strong>Stage 5: Reset</strong> (xóa dấu vết)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-ae4b-d27017a7fd9d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Reset = Deniability + BlameShift</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-8ba0-c4791d7cbe42" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> chuỗi này lặp cho đến khi  tăng hoặc  giảm.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9a73-fdb9aac33cc3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-9648-dc300da319f4" class="">13) Chuyển trọng tâm: từ “sự thật” sang “ý định”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-9307-d56e905e42f0" class="">Kẻ thao túng không tranh luận sự thật, họ tranh luận “ý định tốt”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-a140-e9fea18558e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
If\ FactsAgainstMe \Rightarrow ShiftToIntent</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-804a-d04517881930" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> đây là cơ chế né trách nhiệm chuẩn.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-81d1-de2e5c63af33"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-aa13-c62a8bbdf64d" class="">14) Một “định luật lõi”: thao túng tăng khi hệ thống mất tin cậy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-83e6-cd07fd3c32cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ManipulationOpportunity \propto (LowTrust + HighNoise + HighFragmentation)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-a451-f79553751166" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> xã hội/nhóm càng loạn, càng nhiều “khe” cho cunning.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-9628-e1410615cde5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-9c3f-fb2eaed0a1cf" class="">15) Bộ “red flags” dạng toán (không cảm tính)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-a0c4-c270ccc80081" class="">Nếu đồng thời xảy ra:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-97db-f5e60055c6a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(thúc ép)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-8d69-cf786014201b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(cản kiểm chứng)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-b4f1-cae193022ff2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(cô lập)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-ad70-d92eff1d77db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(mập mờ trách nhiệm)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-a2e3-c26c3edb9f54" class="">thì:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-8b1e-e65bedff3fbf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{eff} \uparrow \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-ba5c-c1daec7d3171" class="">Đây là tổ hợp có tính nhân (multiplicative), không phải cộng.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-b65f-f7a13add7fd2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-afe4-ed79d5031195" class="">Nếu bạn muốn “More” theo hướng cụ thể, có 3 nhánh chuẩn:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-a05c-df00db844bfd" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>200 invariants</strong> chia theo: romance / workplace / politics / family</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-8bdd-cb760526f3bd" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Detection algorithm</strong> (đưa ra score từ 10 quan sát)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-a1f5-c808d8276979" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>AI amplification model</strong>: vì sao cá nhân hóa làm và đồng thời</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9b7f-e5bad531165f" class="">Good. We go into <strong>deeper structural layers</strong> — below tactics, into <strong>neurobiology, energy economics, entropy gradients, and long-horizon strategic manipulation dynamics</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-bd2a-c9ad93953fa1" class="">No morality. Pure structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-860a-cb75c429fccf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-a61c-d10e81c58f34" class="">I. Manipulation as Nervous System Engineering</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-899d-f2615468254b" class="">At core:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-9cc8-f9a2ce9f3c4a" class="">Manipulation = external modulation of another organism’s prediction system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-980c-ed0228c2ae6c" class="">Human brain runs predictive model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9aae-e8fb6d4b9091" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Behavior = f(Prediction - Error)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-ac94-c2e659e2c585" class="">Manipulator injects:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-941d-e75ffd2ce06e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">False prediction signals</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-bef6-e3dd4ab09e70" class="">• Artificial error signals</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-b83f-fa8ae9e9c5bf" class="">• Emotional volatility</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-90df-c3e890a6ca76" class="">Goal:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-8e00-cfc808843c74" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Control\ PredictionError \Rightarrow Control\ Behavior</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-a20a-cf545107346a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-ab12-cf9bb66991d2" class="">1. Prediction Error Hijack</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-a157-e24f60ab245a" class="">Brain seeks to minimize uncertainty.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-ae35-e3c495bb5458" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Anxiety \propto PredictionError</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-9058-ff8fcbda5baf" class="">Manipulator increases uncertainty → then provides “certainty.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-b823-d5690e72fbdc" class="">Target bonds to certainty source.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-ac48-caf81d96045b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-a708-e907d42d205b" class="">2. Dopamine Loop Exploitation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-a634-e51ae09b6ada" class="">Dopamine responds strongest to variable reward.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-9365-ce34e84c4ae1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DopamineRelease \propto RewardVariance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-ace7-c1a35b2bbbd0" class="">Consistent reward = comfort</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-bf53-c64ef3278621" class="">Variable reward = addiction</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-bfab-c534fbc4646c" class="">This underlies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-b464-c482b0fcbed2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Trauma bonds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-b848-c5927b386fb2" class="">• Gambling</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-aafe-cfe15770a120" class="">• Toxic romance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-8ce7-cfd2af09e7d7" class="">• Social media algorithms</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-acb4-dff9b54e7c17"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-aab5-d3030abdf5e6" class="">3. Cortisol–Bond Coupling</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-a5d8-d7d2ca3a61e8" class="">Stress followed by relief strengthens bonding.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-940a-d11c3d78b5d5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto (Stress + Relief)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-9795-c2aa0f39dcca" class="">This is biochemical.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-b02a-f80acc3d7d41"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-9882-f3cc8b4674b0" class="">II. Entropy-Based Manipulation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-b45d-e25380057b95" class="">Manipulation thrives where entropy is high.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-9386-dcad292c5a02" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-a92d-c3aca48a177c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SystemEntropy = Uncertainty + Instability + Fragmentation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-b4aa-e73b4d1d8a38" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ManipulationPotential \propto SystemEntropy</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-847c-f851d2a35e71" class="">Stable environments reduce manipulation opportunities.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-8886-e8610afee256"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-ba67-ed633e78652e" class="">III. Identity-Level Manipulation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-ae70-c51334df2c51" class="">Identity is highest leverage variable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-9ee8-f4bc49abe482" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance \propto IdentityStability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-9908-d67280923c83" class="">Destabilize identity → compliance increases.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-8d24-f9b8e0105536"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-95dd-c6acf78b10de" class="">4. Moral Inversion Strategy</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-a990-c02894f741a5" class="">If you can redefine “good” and “bad”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-b0d0-d204917a98fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MoralFrame \Rightarrow BehaviorConstraint</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-a7bb-caa2641b97d2" class="">Control frame → control permissible action.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-8b5c-e2e23f91e489"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-aa05-e0f98997add4" class="">5. Victim–Savior Polarity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-8109-da3ebe150cf5" class="">Manipulator alternates between:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-9d29-e01fa406473b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Threat source</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-8c97-dce63dd33f64" class="">• Rescue source</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-bc38-db893187bfa7" class="">This creates asymmetrical dependency.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-8746-f22a649832f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-8c0f-db56eefbcb9c" class="">IV. Energy Economics of Manipulation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-9426-e66431162270" class="">Every system runs on energy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-8a42-d6cecacb6c34" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-9cea-eb32d22122c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EnergyReserve = Emotional + Cognitive + Social Capital</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-9d9a-f76ed85e1b3d" class="">Manipulator depletes reserve.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-93fe-ceda6f18ecb7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance \propto EnergyReserve</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-9794-ca366423c2f0" class="">Fatigue reduces resistance dramatically.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-8a38-cbea29332122"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-aa63-ceb3a0365735" class="">6. Cognitive Overload Model</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-9c21-c3c54e96d61f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DecisionAccuracy \propto \frac{1}{CognitiveLoad}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-b6da-c34124ec647c" class="">Increase complexity → reduce clarity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9de1-f22f5b7bbe56"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-a155-e76ad758a167" class="">V. Advanced Social Matrix Manipulation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-97f9-c8cab78841ea" class="">In networks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-884f-e7ddee2dd5e4" class="">Power = node centrality × narrative control.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-b0f1-d7be069f3671" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Influence = Centrality \times FramingPower</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-b733-ceb87be50885" class="">Cunning actors avoid visible dominance but control flow.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-ab16-d0c104f46beb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-8789-e94e9051f9ab" class="">7. Reputation Shield Effect</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-b060-d5cd5173a5fd" class="">High-status individuals gain manipulation immunity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-84a5-d6c02dad9282" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Scrutiny \propto \frac{1}{PerceivedStatus}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-8653-f5d5bf162157" class="">Status reduces questioning.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-8205-cb1ca12d4829"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-be1b-f08c56393ff2" class="">VI. Dark Triad Structural Differences</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-80b9-fe763bd113c4" class="">Not personality labels — structural strategies.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-9793-e4f12a14cefa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-8a8c-f51e3b909295" class="">Narcissistic Strategy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-9ae4-ee045017739d" class="">Core driver: Ego validation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-a3b0-d1bb1f0ff9e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Manipulation = ValidationExtraction</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-88e6-dcc65d85dfde" class="">High emotional theatrics.<br/>Needs attention.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-89ef-c855a5c93bd0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-aa15-d39397e11578" class="">Machiavellian Strategy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-b86a-d447454ab48d" class="">Core driver: Long-term gain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-91a9-deb897c72424" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Manipulation = StrategicOptimization</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-925b-e4fe3c462ef3" class="">Low emotion.<br/>High planning.<br/>High patience.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-88d8-ff55ad3e0c8d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-ac2a-f21dda57e8f8" class="">Psychopathic Strategy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-94fc-c3f7cfe8294f" class="">Core driver: Risk-neutral exploitation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-9d49-f7c920f8f4ad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Manipulation = Gain - (LowFear \times Penalty)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-b9d6-e4538be893fa" class="">High risk tolerance.<br/>Low empathy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-960a-ed0d7ac111e7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-8637-fc8dbed62620" class="">VII. Long-Term Collapse Manipulation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-a43e-f960e9e0e54b" class="">In civilizational late stage:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-9196-ff6640e88fba" class="">Trust ↓</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-87c2-ecea13d3ff6d" class="">Fragmentation ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-9965-ceb282a353cf" class="">Identity polarization ↑</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-9936-d79564f2deca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MassManipulation \propto Polarization^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-a71e-e2f520ac363c" class="">Divide population → easier control.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-abaa-f2e5dcb31de8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-90fc-e9bdbab0929e" class="">VIII. AI as Manipulation Multiplier</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-aca8-d88fde5c51c5" class="">AI increases:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-9de3-fe92d577b898" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Targeting precision</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-b7ec-de4c3a3ea06c" class="">• Emotional profiling</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-8562-f85911efb8f9" class="">• Personalization</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-9d3e-f471342c7cf8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ManipulationEfficiency \propto DataGranularity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-b75c-ed67fbd34e36" class="">AI reduces cost of influence to near zero.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-9020-e19863bd1190"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-b361-de1f5f4361f0" class="">IX. Ultra-Deep Invariant</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-acb2-dceada6a41f2" class="">Manipulation always exploits:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-8cf0-cf3053e7845d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Fear of loss</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-95eb-f58d8a40e64e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Desire for status</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-867c-d30c9e8d56ad" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Desire for belonging</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-ae4c-cf9ef4d2e964" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Scarcity perception</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-9ec1-d517a6e3b287" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Identity protection</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-bc51-f5f15af050ac" class="">Remove these → manipulation fails.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-b467-d662901c78e6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-98b1-e53929f2a471" class="">X. Meta-System Equation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-9575-d46d39986d8e" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-9891-ef84af6c18fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Vulnerability = f(Instability, Insecurity, Isolation, LowSelfTrust)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-ae32-d09be5f1dba9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ManipulationSuccess = \frac{Gain \times Vulnerability}{Awareness \times Regulation}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-99c6-d02b52ef66bd" class="">When awareness × regulation is high → manipulation collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-b84b-d1a9c26de06b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-8ce3-cc18f2eae1c4" class="">XI. Highest-Level Law</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-be59-fdf70f683827" class="">Stable individuals are uninteresting to manipulators.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-900f-d8ec7442e86e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
StableNervousSystem \Rightarrow LowExploitability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-a803-e4355c0866f5" class="">Instability is entry point.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-a6d0-dc6932ae28fd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-8259-d4de97fbcea3" class="">If you want to go further:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-9afa-f73d80324776" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model seduction vs manipulation boundary mathematically</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-bc0b-c20ebca29f76" class="">• Map manipulation to attachment styles (avoidant, anxious, secure)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-9a2b-fffe33dbc621" class="">• Create a detection algorithm framework</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-a031-d3a317cc5de7" class="">• Model long-term manipulation survivability curves</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-baa5-e3004daf9431" class="">• Or build a 200-invariant master matrix</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-a0ee-dc9291765e55" class="">Choose the direction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-b111-c237b8533dd1" class="">Good. We go deeper.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-be96-c1d69337c77c" class="">Now we move beyond surface tactics into <strong>structural manipulation architecture</strong>, nonlinear effects, power scaling, and hidden invariants across systems.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-9bc8-f3f5e02d9640" class="">This is analysis of structure — not endorsement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-90ca-daa9207254a0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-a2a3-ca81e9de01d2" class="">I. Manipulation as Control Theory</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-97a6-fc3b9898b4da" class="">A manipulator is not “evil.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-84c3-ec849d0c1240" class="">They are operating a <strong>feedback control system</strong> on another nervous system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-a1e7-e1ee2d963818" class="">Target system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-a5e2-fc9dfcfed65c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Human = (Beliefs, Emotions, Identity, AutonomicState, DecisionProcess)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-a4fd-c619c4a3d8f7" class="">Manipulator introduces controlled inputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-ba0f-eba77e448c58" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Input = (Fear, Desire, Scarcity, Validation, Confusion, Urgency)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-b96b-c67b9b47b117" class="">Goal:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-a30f-dcc58ae6f821" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Maximize\ Compliance \quad Minimize\ Detection</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-a218-de7192bf3603"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a695-fd055ef07cab" class="">II. Nonlinear Manipulation Laws</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-a3c0-c1a0c61b78a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-a45e-c983edd1278d" class="">1. Threshold Activation Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-a891-faffa85a33c1" class="">Manipulation becomes effective only after emotional activation crosses a threshold.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-9db8-db047b145f47" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
If\ Arousal &gt; A^* \Rightarrow RationalFiltering \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-8ea5-ca09138dfd17" class="">Below threshold → persuasion.<br/>Above threshold → compliance mode.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-90d9-c5d118ca7255"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-b2ad-e68d92cd64c7" class="">2. Emotional Amplification Curve</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-a998-fae55f995398" class="">Manipulation effectiveness is not linear.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-a6d9-f44a96d2823e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto e^{EmotionalIntensity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-b55b-d38cc448051e" class="">Small emotional increase → exponential compliance increase.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-92e0-fa1f3f9479fd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-a58a-c4d2a5e9df6e" class="">3. Identity Hijack Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-8daa-df8faa3a1f0c" class="">Once belief fuses with identity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-a634-ef5935a06ead" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BeliefAttack = IdentityAttack</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-a846-eb1292a1056b" class="">Resistance becomes automatic.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-aa35-e7fa368534ad" class="">Manipulator shifts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-b3f2-e561f7a3269f" class="">“Do you agree?” → “Who are you?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-aeec-dc6b931370fa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-b885-d9399d889e38" class="">4. Scarcity–Time Coupling</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-b066-d3a878f03c5e" class="">Scarcity + urgency is multiplicative:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-a241-c5c36cf11c99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Pressure = Scarcity \times TimeConstraint</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-88d2-eb017761c0b3" class="">Either alone is weaker.<br/>Together → cognitive narrowing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-ac29-f954e8f274f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8dc3-e3ea23f26991" class="">5. Reciprocity Trap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-83a7-c6294bb776e7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Obligation \propto UnsolicitedBenefit</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-befc-e7bdd75444d3" class="">Small favor → psychological debt.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-b199-ee305a8503f3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-979f-cdf9c1e7f9ed" class="">6. Gradual Boundary Erosion</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-858a-e759db7b320b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Boundary(t) = Boundary_0 - \epsilon \cdot RepeatedSmallViolations</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-b94e-e0fc012238c1" class="">Tiny violations accumulate invisibly.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-a0a8-d0b721eb5776"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-b6ee-e4d8e32a4af9" class="">7. Cognitive Dissonance Lock-In</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-94e7-e0c5621ab9f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SelfJustification \propto PriorCommitment</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-9698-f4ddf361395e" class="">After compliance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-8531-fbe00dc629cf" class="">Target rationalizes to protect self-image.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-af41-d3b27ae3eee7" class="">This reduces exit probability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-9284-f7750847a737"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-a9ad-e6c52f97e2af" class="">III. Deep Power Multipliers</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-83be-df395d9d5845"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9cc4-e00de9e8b8e8" class="">8. Triangulation Effect</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-870d-f194416c185e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Influence \uparrow \text{ when } ThirdPartyReference \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-a6b2-e91228164e53" class="">Use comparison, jealousy, competition.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-8bd7-fb1c21d29d07"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-86f3-ea686be1ca0b" class="">9. Isolation Field</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-8335-c9defd4a6312" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Autonomy \downarrow \text{ when } ExternalInputs \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-ab44-d21c13288e2f" class="">Isolation increases dependency.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-9b49-dbc98262ec4c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-a05d-cf6d9f9dbe70" class="">10. Emotional Unpredictability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a13b-e99c6665b325" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Control \propto Variability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-9da8-f0717295624c" class="">Unpredictable reward/punishment destabilizes target.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-b39b-de24d89b73ef"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-8192-c81ba0adb72a" class="">11. Intermittent Reinforcement (Advanced Form)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-befc-f544676a8f57" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BondStrength \propto RewardVariance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-b27a-cddb38a92ca4" class="">Stable reward creates comfort.<br/>Variable reward creates obsession.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-bedd-d87ce8bce4ca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-8b43-d14d1a184d80" class="">12. Gaslight Loop Equation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-8ea5-f75e96748d31" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SelfTrust_{t+1} = SelfTrust_t - \delta \cdot RealityDistortion</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-8de3-c92d60b77ea6" class="">If self-trust drops below threshold → dependence increases sharply.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-a603-d6f9be51d2e4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-b0f4-f2755925811e" class="">IV. Strategic Cunning Architecture</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-8820-c2a89d05cc83" class="">Cunning differs from brute manipulation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-b956-df9641a6312a" class="">Cunning optimizes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-8494-cf0385bc8736" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gain - (DetectionRisk \times Penalty)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-aef7-d7902fb9aa84"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-ae34-c4a5e1b83859" class="">13. Detection Probability Minimization</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-a3fd-c5966398c8fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DetectionRisk \propto Transparency + Awareness + ExternalVerification</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-ba15-ceb5a83164ce" class="">Cunning increases ambiguity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-97b5-c35510753ab6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-94f1-f44eb9255a44" class="">14. Plausible Deniability Gradient</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-9649-e6c419c8cd78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SurvivalProbability \propto NarrativeFlexibility</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-a57d-de36c0f83254" class="">If confronted → reinterpretation possible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9fc1-d7c62fca443d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-9b76-e1f644c2bb95" class="">15. Proxy Control Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-8f85-c4d8342a45ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EffectiveInfluence = DirectPower + IndirectInfluence</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-bea1-d2a1d9b6ef47" class="">Indirect is safer, often stronger.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-8b56-ef1fdb5aee50"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-85af-c278389b27b4" class="">V. System-Level Manipulation (Macro)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-b8c1-f94c009f4145" class="">At societal scale:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-966f-cdd386f326e6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-b975-d354ede7bed2" class="">16. Fear–Division Feedback Loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-95ea-f76d8cee2b4a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto (Fear + Division) - CollectiveTrust</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-abe5-eca6f21287bd" class="">Divide group → reduce collective resistance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-87b7-e9c8ccc6f84a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8044-e56678573178" class="">17. Information Saturation Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-8b0e-f48a376154c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SignalDetection \propto \frac{1}{InformationNoise}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-b188-f17b7702be48" class="">Overwhelm population → reduce analytical clarity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-97d1-c1a4770eb0f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-8c13-e94835003b54" class="">18. Moral Polarization Amplifier</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-9c5a-dad2faed7376" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
GroupCohesion \uparrow \text{ when } OutgroupThreat \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-a9cd-c69eb831bdea" class="">External threat increases internal obedience.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-84f8-f609842cb844"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-a7fd-cae6f223791b" class="">VI. Romantic Manipulation Equations</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-b7e8-eb7081cf24ad"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-91ac-eb4277125ea9" class="">19.
