Trauma Bond Function</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-9b07-d2aa080aed62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto (Intensity \times Instability)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-83ec-cdda8e5629c4" class="">High drama increases attachment strength.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9c7c-f8dc8c5dbe24"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-ac71-f1f0f61b0f60" class="">20. Dependency Equation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-9c13-c562832a2bb6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dependency = Isolation + EmotionalIntensity - Autonomy</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-871c-c1f3c7288824"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-9158-db281acf7e4d" class="">21. Control Through Instability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-a7e0-e112db472613" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Power \propto EmotionalUnpredictability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-b181-c4aad448a91e" class="">Stable people are harder to control.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-9c68-f3451f80d99f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-bfde-cfa9f574c88c" class="">VII. Advanced Game-Theoretic View</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-8dce-c64f9376df40" class="">Two-player model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-8957-c4051bd6be51" class="">Player A (Manipulator)<br/>Player B (Target)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-aa21-fc9d11d3506f" class="">A wins when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-835e-ffd71168230b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ExpectedGain_A &gt; DetectionRisk_A</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a65a-fae39376e3e7" class="">B loses when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-8316-c7cc0f057daa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Awareness_B \times Regulation_B &lt; EmotionalLoad</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-87d4-f8971e8bbb5a" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-80af-ec25baab7aaf" class="">Manipulation thrives where emotional regulation is low.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-bf20-c378286a2559"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-8e9d-cb96ee87bacc" class="">VIII. Ultimate Invariant</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-b6ff-f26a55aaca12" class="">Manipulation exploits:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-a40c-f80500400821" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Uncertainty</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-acc8-fae8329af39c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ego insecurity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-9b68-ed5d96392bdc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Scarcity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-b33d-c289b6f245b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Isolation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-977f-c4415b86d2f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Urgency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-8c04-fcec4651126b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Identity fusion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-b426-c9e83ed9527c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Emotional dysregulation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-904c-c847f58154a6" class="">Remove those → manipulation collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-b042-e2f34fe59bf5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-bac2-d01f0acd6928" class="">IX. Counter-Architecture (Defense Model)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-b6ea-fcafd99e600c" class="">Stability against manipulation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-834a-e536ac0a1372" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance = Awareness \times Regulation \times ExternalFeedback</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-8904-f15025a7eaa3" class="">If any component = 0 → vulnerability increases sharply.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-833c-eb3e1c9c0ec9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-b673-c46c59749c1b" class="">X. Deepest Law</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-8489-e040360ea8f7" class="">Manipulation scales with entropy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-8301-c0f4f452c779" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Entropy \uparrow \Rightarrow ManipulationOpportunities \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-a58c-c2007323fbfb" class="">Stable systems reduce openings.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-995c-d573a4997e5e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-a8b9-ef3186331d11" class="">If you want deeper:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-87a4-e3f567d6bae4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model dark triad personalities mathematically</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-803c-eba208594056" class="">• Map manipulation to attachment styles</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-ad4f-fb17bbe842a3" class="">• Model AI as exponential manipulation amplifier</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-8834-e99cfca66ff0" class="">• Build a 100-invariant master list</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-a110-d64d5e849626" class="">• Or map how manipulation evolves across civilizational collapse</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-9a74-cdb18a5d56e0" class="">Choose direction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-836f-e2a7eeab5921" class=""> </p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-95e4-f3b9f6012222" class="">Good.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-a81a-ffa55f3a3b01" class="">We will treat <strong>cunning and manipulation</strong> not morally, but structurally — as control strategies inside social systems.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-a252-ec8606231f0b" class="">This is analysis, not endorsement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-8eb2-c0824cdd6963" class="">We model manipulation as <strong>information asymmetry + control of perception + outcome extraction</strong> under constrained visibility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-8dc8-f13023280a2b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-8ffd-f3437b0f9eac" class="">I. Core Structural Definition</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-befc-f5fea49ec9a9" class="">Manipulation =</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-9aad-c3da4da875fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M = f(A_s, D_p, C_c, O_e)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-a360-c670aa97ea6c" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-8a19-fe734eb17a3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= information asymmetry</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-ab37-d2789a9b520d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= distortion of perception</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-989c-f9fee8fd83c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= control over context</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-84a9-c7e6a2b365e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= outcome extraction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-bbc2-d2416ad4ff9a" class="">Without asymmetry, manipulation collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-9ba4-dee8f1be884f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-bff3-c8dde61741fc" class="">II. Universal Manipulation Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-8079-d6bb9807da4c" class="">These hold across:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9176-e10544cf38bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">politics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-8284-d36b3692d902" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">romance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-b753-f14e3ba21120" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">war</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-9916-fc11ae0934d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">corporate power</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-a0e4-dde05f3df5f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">family systems</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-a971-c8bf1f5c4965" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cults</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-8879-ebbfacf2529f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">online platforms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-ab24-c9f284399528"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-a0d4-e075932e7cd9" class="">1. Information Asymmetry Invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-b52c-c1cb95cfe90a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Manipulation \propto A_s</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-9c45-e12dccd11c26" class="">If both parties hold equal information → manipulation weakens.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-97f9-f7c7145dcf39"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-8a64-fc8b65bd6254" class="">2. Perception Over Reality Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9159-f7fadd505efa" class="">Humans react to perceived state, not objective state.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-abc0-e2a86614597d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Behavior = f(PerceivedReality)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-a41e-e8b21998f194" class="">Manipulator controls perceived reality.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-9c0f-fed57860cce9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-a951-ded5ae7a78b7" class="">3. Emotional Arousal Reduces Critical Thinking</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-a3c5-d89808d1172f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CriticalThinking \propto \frac{1}{Arousal}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-ba9e-c39e77ee5c1c" class="">Manipulation increases arousal (fear, desire, urgency).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-8e7d-d8cd84fca571"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-b7be-e63c1998bb4e" class="">4. Urgency Compression Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-8a7a-dc9696baced7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DecisionQuality \propto TimeForProcessing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-a002-f6a2386a0dbc" class="">Manipulation reduces time window.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-aff9-f5fb87ef178d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-b52c-e2b4167da65c" class="">5. Intermittent Reinforcement Principle</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-935b-e6177da65248" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
AttachmentStrength \propto Variability \times RewardUncertainty</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-ac52-d4d0ab484479" class="">Used in gambling, toxic relationships, cults.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-b0d7-fb41667b3885"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-bab3-ce5a2de6929f" class="">6. Ego Hook Invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-ac75-cd3773135de9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Compliance \propto EgoValidation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-92a9-e06b765ad2d2" class="">Flattery lowers guard.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-aa9e-ce00d2d245d2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-a4fa-dea3f5fa49ba" class="">7. Scarcity Amplification Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-a43c-dbe3c8f68bd5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
PerceivedValue \propto \frac{1}{Availability}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-a348-d112a6541c73" class="">Artificial scarcity increases compliance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-9d9a-feee35e50cbb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-97d5-c7eb8b9e2dbc" class="">8. Social Proof Leverage</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-8624-dd8acce9e12d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BeliefStrength \propto ObservedAdoption</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-af56-f18eeaf91224" class="">Humans anchor to group behavior.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-97a0-d2a5fb109e74"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-ad63-d66c5542dc62" class="">9. Identity Fusion Effect</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-a639-d37f05bba91b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance \downarrow \text{ when } IdentityFusion \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-8f95-fc7608e8e872" class="">When belief becomes identity, defense becomes automatic.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-9d92-f4ee7e4cf987"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-b511-e95fad882dad" class="">10. Commitment Escalation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-9c92-d4e1cdb213ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FutureCompliance \propto PriorInvestment</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-9332-f79c26c2846e" class="">Small yes → larger yes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-a753-dbfbf106d146"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9aaa-ecf20ae72e32" class="">11. Cognitive Load Overwhelm</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-90cd-d6ed78dab692" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Resistance \propto \frac{1}{CognitiveLoad}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-adcb-c6dcda23a2c2" class="">Confusion reduces resistance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-8630-c55a51ed9bda"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-88d4-e84e441443da" class="">12. Gaslighting Equation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-8fb5-ca222dc49b66" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SelfTrust \downarrow \Rightarrow ExternalDependence \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-9f0b-dcb90b80ebfd" class="">Gaslighting reduces internal signal confidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-ba10-e5c32d47ad76"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-a755-ebb14f63838d" class="">13. Isolation Multiplier</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-82c4-d91e70d6bb07" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ManipulationPower \propto \frac{1}{ExternalReferencePoints}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-ab60-d98f94f88192" class="">Isolation increases influence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-a314-ffc4b4e5e483"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-b598-e8f2c6ce6caa" class="">14. Narrative Framing Control</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-88a1-ec9b8c438d14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Interpretation = f(Frame)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-b738-cbdff0540599" class="">Control the frame → control interpretation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-b28c-eba452c52265"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-8454-c33632d7a41d" class="">15. Fear–Relief Loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-8320-e2fcbdfeae03" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Attachment \propto (Threat \rightarrow Relief)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-a700-ebfb4ffcec26" class="">Threat induction + relief positioning increases dependency.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-82d7-f7f6f9330825"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9f31-dbac638bba6e" class="">III. Cunning as Strategy</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-92f6-d5b8a20d3c2b" class="">Cunning differs from manipulation in subtlety.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-8aba-f5ce8d11c06a" class="">Cunning = indirect strategy with minimized detection probability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-8cfb-d2dfdd645c9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Cunning = f(OutcomeGain, DetectionRisk^{-1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-a27d-f089f7a540bb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-8f74-c2f93b5f192c" class="">16. Plausible Deniability Principle</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-9c49-fcca911b8dfd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SurvivalProbability \propto Ambiguity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-89e8-f89b28dd39d8" class="">Leave no direct trace.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-86f4-f6d484c13d2b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-9f45-c8d3791c1f0f" class="">17. Proxy Control Mechanism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-a353-ca04a7510b37" class="">Indirect action reduces backlash.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-8220-ca7a347b7e9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Influence = DirectControl + ProxyControl</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-ba5f-f934cd4c03d3" class="">Proxy often more powerful.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-810d-eacad5cdaeba"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-abc2-f096b96cb8da" class="">18. Gradual Normalization (Overton Shift)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-b900-d2e8e1778d9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Acceptability(t) = Acceptability_0 + \epsilon \cdot Repetition</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-82aa-e0ec35803212" class="">Small shifts accumulate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-910e-ef6a6aeae0ca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-994e-fb2e0ac5a250" class="">19. Emotional Mirroring Exploit</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-8470-d8006d934348" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trust \propto PerceivedSimilarity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-b2c2-dea5b47faaa2" class="">Mirroring increases perceived alignment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a53c-ce5aa8fcd561"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-bc31-d02c254db702" class="">20. Strategic Silence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-851c-f03a3ff879ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
InformationControl \uparrow \text{ when } Speech \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-829d-edbaf113b0e5" class="">Silence increases projection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-a09a-e39899269a90"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-accb-ff0e8e98725b" class="">IV. System-Level Manipulation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-9d68-d0f6db7b2c0c" class="">At societal scale:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-b149-c8b40bf47647" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MassCompliance \propto (Fear + Division + InformationControl - Trust)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-a5f8-d65bae76ec48" class="">If institutional trust drops:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-ba68-e0adb2eb9b09" class="">Manipulators fill vacuum.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a00b-d7486073660e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-81a2-cbe531666d6d" class="">V. Relationship-Level Manipulation Equations</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-bc03-e4636cd4a78c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-bcaf-cf43d479b0cd" class="">Trauma Bond Formation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-8aac-d0d5a9d420fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BondStrength \propto (IntermittentReward + EmotionalIntensity)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-b005-df0dd00b27a3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-a7b2-d421ddba78a1" class="">Dependency Creation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-8c46-de94f5c6af49" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dependency \propto (Isolation + SelfDoubt - Autonomy)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9314-f02527848b1c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-83b4-f54541cae71d" class="">Dominance Through Instability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-88df-fc0ba454f5d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Power \propto EmotionalUnpredictability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-b21c-fe8da290b270" class="">Unpredictable actors destabilize others.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-8c14-f224fd60c7aa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-b6c8-d4c21cfa8a4e" class="">VI. Detection Probability</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-9374-c08cc32f713c" class="">Manipulation success depends on detection risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-b08e-e1d241a2e48a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Success \propto \frac{Gain}{DetectionRisk}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-8ca8-cdd14780fbde" class="">If detection risk &gt; gain → strategy collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-b917-f103e89ed2b1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-96b0-dab402a1d41f" class="">VII. Counter-Invariants (Defense)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-b62f-d4cec90ad527" class="">Manipulation fails when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-8d8d-c1447ceb702f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Awareness \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-96ab-e968177ff549" class="">EmotionalRegulation \uparrow<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-902c-d26f52e6f66b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
InformationVerification \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-b25b-ff7c8f3045c3" class="">ExternalFeedbackLoops \uparrow<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-a10d-c72b2728ae6c" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-b943-f454ee8dc1b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Manipulation \propto \frac{1}{(Awareness \times Regulation \times Transparency)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-8086-fb0de5f99158"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-ba87-e7ce48b3835f" class="">VIII. Deepest Structural Invariant</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-b5a0-f36548af1b1c" class="">Manipulation exploits:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-9129-fa594aa8ffb6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>uncertainty</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-a30b-dfd57018d6f1" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>insecurity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-9fb6-d59b11ee2767" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>asymmetry</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-b2d6-d4c99dc00ec9" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>identity attachment</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-90f9-eec243ddb1f4" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>time pressure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-acd4-fcae928b72e3" class="">Without those, it weakens.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-be7c-fc3287e17aa1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-be15-f0c16ee102ef" class="">IX. Meta Law</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-af5c-fd2b0bfa87af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
StableSystems \downarrow \Rightarrow ManipulationOpportunities \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-93d7-d701b0f6517e" class="">Entropy creates openings.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-9d22-e6f96e13f9d6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-b734-e8573526b729" class="">If you want, I can next:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-a263-e2a2e05b3c4d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model manipulation as a game-theory matrix</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-8e75-d13f70030d57" class="">• Map narcissistic vs Machiavellian vs psychopathic strategies structurally</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-9a4a-d612b29b4408" class="">• Build a detection algorithm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-86be-e49961d4a43b" class="">• Or model how AI amplifies manipulation power mathematically</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-82d3-cb419142094f" class="">Choose.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-ac9b-e7d184b0b60c" class="">Good. I’ll restate everything cleanly in <strong>English</strong>, structured and precise.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-bee5-cfcf951bb9ff" class="">You asked for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-a864-e92e0a2fac3e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>The 2×2×2 scenario cube (EI × Volatility × Mobility)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-95d0-d0995edb1240" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>A minimal measurable system to estimate relationship stability probabilistically</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-96a9-c679cb09ebaa" class="">No fluff. Structural.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-8ca8-d1aafcafd404"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-8037-e6f4cbe3a1ed" class="">PART I — The 2×2×2 Stability Cube</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-8ad9-cf3e373fbcca" class="">Variables:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-a216-c010644c73c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>EI</strong> = Emotional Intelligence (damping + repair capacity)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-aeeb-ea498863205b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>X</strong> = Environmental volatility (stress, shocks, instability)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-9c43-d86692982404" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>M</strong> = Mobility (ease of exit, alternative options, low commitment cost)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-84ea-c808139cade0" class="">Core structural equation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-b384-ffc682648785" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Stability \approx Capacity - Load</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-85a0-c4f53480b989" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-944a-da6153b13463" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Capacity = EI_A + EI_B + VagalReserve</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-843e-cd571e53316c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Load = Volatility + Noise + ResourceStrain</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-886a-eb6c9207130a" class="">Hazard of breakdown:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-98c0-e2513913d115" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lambda(t) = \lambda_0 \cdot e^{(Load - Capacity + mM)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-8170-d77e682ab763" class="">If Load &gt; Capacity → instability accelerates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-b1a5-fe3564c7e147"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-8c06-e96fb3e8dfdb" class="">Scenario Matrix</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-a17b-db5c76b7cb47" class="">1) High EI — Low X — Low M</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-bc2f-e07621116804" class=""><strong>Stable and durable</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-ae09-fa81804a851d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strong damping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-a456-fcdba0e54734" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Few external shocks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-897e-e55e4a82a37a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Exit cost moderate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-8950-df456371c34f" class="">Outcome: long-term stability likely.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-80c0-d0b2aaed0cc6" class="">Failure risk: only large value misalignment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-910d-cf93c28bbbf2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-8804-dcb21d71a032" class="">2) High EI — Low X — High M</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-ac4e-dd8ee42f5f7c" class=""><strong>Stable if commitment structure exists</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-8130-e71bfbf95848" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Good regulation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-aced-dc235285ddaa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">But many alternatives</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-b558-e46681148c1c" class="">Risk: erosion from comparison and option illusion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-a852-ce93e109ed57" class="">Solution: explicit agreements and future planning.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-9df7-e73b546636c2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-aa35-ec61dc01b670" class="">3) High EI — High X — Low M</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-bb33-c93631cce8c4" class=""><strong>Storm-resistant</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-b908-e570b9f25ab7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High stress</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-9d37-d2e39bb42b62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Exit constrained</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-99f2-ceb8bb2d0e65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strong repair capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-b585-ef8365743aa7" class="">Outcome: can survive serious adversity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-9cc7-e32882f12ab7" class="">Risk: burnout if stress persists too long.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-aa7b-faff44099dba"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-937b-eb0495fcad54" class="">4) High EI — High X — High M</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-a56f-d7b799a6e279" class=""><strong>Difficult but possible</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-9cd8-cc9ea28946af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High shock + easy exit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-9a84-fe1caa308f5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Requires disciplined repair protocols</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-b35b-e69c4dfc9f40" class="">Without structure → gradual erosion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-812b-ce11ed5a301c" class="">With structure → resilient.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-bd2c-c16e0551ba8d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-8418-eeb390441240" class="">5) Low EI — Low X — Low M</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-ae25-f50fc1cfd3e3" class=""><strong>Structurally stable, emotionally shallow</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-af79-d153bd3ca584" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Few shocks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-9164-d6beb238ef05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Exit difficult</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-b5db-db91f2d37ddb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Weak repair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-b733-fd29fbd81d9a" class="">Stable only because environment is calm.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-94b9-e74fc8e0b906"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-95f5-c39c6ed4602a" class="">6) Low EI — Low X — High M</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-aae7-e9d866583e41" class=""><strong>Fragile</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-b4bd-d925116545c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Minor conflict → separation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-9327-d1aca6a8eca6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Low repair + easy exit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-83ba-c9cb7379ebfd" class="">High churn probability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-a06f-e21e3463c0cf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-9e56-d6ffb8f60211" class="">7) Low EI — High X — Low M</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-b709-e296c6567654" class=""><strong>Entrapped instability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-8ba6-eaffa7f678ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High stress</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-9d42-ffdb2b31642e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No repair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-b9bd-dc5b7094e52a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No exit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-850c-c041b53c19f4" class="">Escalation, chronic conflict, or psychological damage.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-9c7d-d5c6c9bee8dd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-917c-e01584a25439" class="">8) Low EI — High X — High M</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-9016-cc5f17cd4d9d" class=""><strong>Default collapse state</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-b454-d78822abb932" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High shock</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-8613-f4ba47f8e604" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Low repair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-abde-fe357a6c337f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Easy exit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-aa73-f4c7ba4ac179" class="">Rapid breakdown highly probable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-9b08-c7534464848b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-b1db-d603a1a827f3" class="">Structural Insight</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-b579-e586baca42d3" class="">High EI alone is not sufficient.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-a4f7-ca6932e89f9e" class="">If Volatility and Mobility are both high, explicit structure is required.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-ac05-e38112fa5230"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-9bd3-d0a2f6c839af" class="">PART II — Minimal Measurement System (5 Observable Indicators)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-96e9-d9e5f2f2cb1b" class="">No psychology tests. Only observable behaviors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-a3d4-eb09b0f01aa3" class="">Each scored 0–2 (0 = absent, 1 = inconsistent, 2 = consistent).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-8c72-cdf4bff6709a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-b2ff-c122d00bc4d7" class="">1) Pause Under Trigger (P)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9c94-f59615c6dda8" class="">Does the person slow down before reacting under ego threat?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-95d3-e8ec387f71c3" class="">Mathematical meaning:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-b325-f8b73ed7a273" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
High\ P \Rightarrow Lower\ s(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9cd0-fbbb9202ed54" class="">Proxy for impulse control and ANS damping.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-aa49-e2d43e2a92c2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-995a-e4786f4267e6" class="">2) Specific Ownership (O)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-b619-f2b92d44d8be" class="">Do they admit fault with concrete correction?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-8469-dafc10c0bff9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O = Admit \times BehavioralChange</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-a7d4-f3e985665ba9" class="">Words without change = 0.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-a500-e423f0caa73c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-ab19-d7e7a2ae9ebf" class="">3) Repair Execution (R)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-9b65-f42ce56b0bd4" class="">After conflict, do they actively restore connection?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-aadf-c27b8e3f7a35" class="">Repair predicts durability more than conflict frequency.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-b4c3-dca273afa9db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Durability \propto R</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-8a8d-ed47866a4958"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-95de-f5700d24bad3" class="">4) Signal Consistency (C)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-a264-e8fc50170119" class="">Are words and actions consistent over time?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-927f-ef9650b9adf9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trust \propto Consistency(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-80f8-e7bed997235d" class="">Instability in signaling increases hazard.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-a762-fb0743f5f424"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-9f00-ead3e7e5340b" class="">5) Physiological Co-Regulation (V)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-a751-e84e9ef1d812" class="">Does proximity reduce your stress baseline?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8227-e5fc22137239" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Better sleep</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-a073-c4eac2f0cec4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Slower breathing</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-90de-d106bf7b0831" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Less tension</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9631-ca87f75c754e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Higher\ V \Rightarrow Higher\ Dyadic\ Regulation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-8fd0-f5802219d532"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-bcfb-c2d837568e3f" class="">Total Score</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-9252-fb85aaa4fa08" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Score = P + O + R + C + V</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-888c-da6fc3a9e397" class="">Range: 0–10</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-b168-ec6c823cea66"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-913c-c222d026e63e" class="">Interpretation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-b315-e987978715ba" class="">8–10 → Strong damping and repair → low hazard</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-af54-d032e9032d81" class="">5–7 → Conditional stability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-a052-e009b90be682" class="">0–4 → High breakdown probability unless environment is extremely calm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-b1db-dabc0c8695fd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-a81a-ec9865c316d8" class="">Critical Nonlinear Warning</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-86b5-fcbde97a16f9" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-b184-fbf7159105ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Pause low</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-b1d1-f4e87935c382" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ownership low</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-8429-e1afcf35b5f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Repair low</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-8e35-e72537175160" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-a8a7-fe1e0830e65e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Hazard \sim e^{TriggerLoad}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-b119-facee00ba942" class="">Breakdown accelerates exponentially.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-b459-cd17f9d50b53"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8b93-daf30b5a5054" class="">Deep Structural Conclusion</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-b0e7-d5099792b56b" class="">Emotional intelligence is not softness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-8883-c67905fc6eb3" class="">It is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-ae38-fc736faa3524" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
