StabilityUnderPerturbation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-9f55-e33e2ab05f7c" class="">Relationships fail when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-808b-ed9c1eb034c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Load &gt; RepairCapacity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-89eb-e5495c01d9b2" class="">They survive when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-bca9-e2e85f8d52fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RepairCapacity &gt; Load</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-a290-daa5cfac6d10" class="">Mobility amplifies whatever already exists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-b876-cfebb6807c07"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-8bc2-fbae2ff266ab" class="">If you want next layer, I can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-9ef3-f4697b3bdef7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Convert this into a decision algorithm</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-a949-f205934ca09f" class="">• Build a probabilistic simulation model</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-94c4-ff5d6fe006e9" class="">• Map this to long-term marriage survival curves</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-98a6-e870bfc9f7f4" class="">• Or model your specific pairing mathematically</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-88db-e69539962cf2" class="">Choose direction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-ad7e-c90fb5724a14" class="">Good. We will formalize <strong>Emotional Intelligence (EI)</strong> as a dynamical system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-bd4c-dc4e69286c46" class="">Not motivational.<br/>Not soft.<br/>Structural.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-9d83-fdd4e6549a85" class="">Below are <strong>core invariants + equations</strong> describing EI as a regulatory architecture.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-bb5a-e3c28ec94472"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-a068-c606a5df5a4d" class="">I. Foundational System Model</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9965-e2a33d705279" class="">Define emotional intelligence as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-bbfa-f3478f0b10d0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EI = f(Aw, Reg, Emp, Rep, Int)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-aa86-edd49d4e9dfb" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-a6c9-eb45a483558d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Aw</strong> = self-awareness accuracy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-9cf5-d201c6979cee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Reg</strong> = regulation bandwidth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-8507-dceb461db798" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Emp</strong> = empathic precision</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-8364-fc25cdfd71a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Rep</strong> = repair capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-8e70-cfec9781649c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Int</strong> = integrity (signal consistency over time)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-94e2-f22a699d19f7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-b35b-f5cdb92322ad" class="">II. Core Invariants + Equations</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-ae60-fc6695f5d303"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-a2bb-f45676326e7b" class="">1. Awareness Precedes Regulation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-97f8-e2eff2408177" class="">You cannot regulate what you cannot detect.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-8eec-ed2bdd65b876" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Reg \leq Aw</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-b0d9-dc9ea340af2c" class="">If awareness is low, regulation ceiling is low.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-819b-f836f44fa653" class="">Invariant: Increasing awareness expands regulation potential.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-b8cb-d97336de7834"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-94e1-d436ae141c4e" class="">2. Granularity Reduces Reactivity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-af50-c9b87f1e7c50" class="">Emotional granularity (G) reduces impulsive amplitude.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-89be-ea19fd6eda63" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Reactivity \propto \frac{1}{G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-af2e-fb0ea5682d87" class="">More precise labeling → lower volatility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-8a70-f3bb4a46a8c1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-bcf4-fd2241a44705" class="">3. Suppression Increases Load</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-9e42-c684214c7c78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Suppression \uparrow \Rightarrow PhysiologicalLoad \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-9fb3-e5b8815a3e9a" class="">Long term:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-a6ee-c55f156d598a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ChronicSuppression \rightarrow Dysregulation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-b303-e26e1864f1c8" class="">Invariant across cultures.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-ba18-fb457997db1f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-a1df-c204ee6c4f83" class="">4. Regulation Bandwidth Determines Stability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-bf3d-f323cf97c5bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RelationalStability \propto RegBandwidth - TriggerLoad</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-9e87-c164449bc1b2" class="">If triggers exceed bandwidth → collapse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-b7cc-f0004788940c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-ad21-e9bb645ab7e4" class="">5. Recovery Speed &gt; Baseline Calm</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-9412-d33f276d308d" class="">What matters is not calmness, but recovery slope.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-937e-d79718ae5d05" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EI \propto \frac{1}{RecoveryTime}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-85e4-cb5b1cac05b1" class="">Fast recovery predicts maturity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-b3e3-cfcdb4d12bae"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-b584-e0ac9eb9aaf6" class="">6. Projection Increases When Awareness Decreases</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-9edc-fe220a2bd227" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Projection \propto \frac{1}{Aw}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9174-c561785bc548" class="">Low self-awareness → misattribution of internal states.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-b853-ec606552f93f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-a1d6-eb3bc6198120" class="">7. Empathy Accuracy Requires Internal Stability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-84ca-e8d017fd47d0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EmpAccuracy \propto Reg</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-9816-ebbc2432a31a" class="">If dysregulated → empathy becomes distorted.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-98df-cde6882d8fe0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-95f0-f69087cb6738" class="">8. Emotional Entropy Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-bb14-fe3c2593670a" class="">Define emotional entropy (Hₑ):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-88f1-de47de885131" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Hₑ = Variability \times Impulsivity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-a242-e2e5fe3b454a" class="">High EI reduces entropy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-b667-fde6bfea8fae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EI \uparrow \Rightarrow Hₑ \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-8e42-ca7d4e18a2ff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-9834-d165d0439f49" class="">9. Repair Overrides Conflict Frequency</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-aa89-f1c2afeb3f5c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RelationshipDurability \propto RepairCapacity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-a683-c3e5670132b7" class="">Even with high conflict:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-8ce3-cc383a665ebf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HighRepair \Rightarrow StableBond</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-a389-f3d928b91ff3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-a329-f0f014aa0c37" class="">10. Ego Threat Amplification</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-a2b0-e74d0400d6b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EgoFragility \uparrow \Rightarrow Reactivity^2 \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-a2ab-e937ff0a2644" class="">Nonlinear effect.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-8b12-ef5138659c71" class="">Small criticism → large response.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-94c1-d64765391544"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-a1b0-f38392bb019c" class="">11. Nervous System Synchrony</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-b811-ee7b97b318df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BondStrength \propto \frac{CoRegulation}{StressBaseline}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-a2b5-e7d7dcecbb4e" class="">Stable people synchronize better.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-9cea-db716bd89108"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-b73e-c9f622ab26a1" class="">12. Integrity Consistency Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-824b-e7990fb99fc1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trust \propto SignalConsistency(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-abe9-f5d737ffa035" class="">If signals fluctuate unpredictably:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-8e27-e8410717fc99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trust \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-820f-fa3bb7c0d800"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-8955-d3f19c28973d" class="">13. Emotional Latency Invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-a286-da4970d3af7c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ResponseLatency \downarrow \Rightarrow Impulsivity \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-9a6d-e7c363b04321" class="">High EI inserts pause.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-9e8b-fc7c5a52237f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-b367-f111dd7de497" class="">14. Shame Tolerance Predicts Growth</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-982d-c88f6ab8e0a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Growth \propto ShameTolerance</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-a8b1-c666b6c5eedb" class="">Low shame tolerance → defensive stagnation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-b2c5-ddeb0bd12b4d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-a343-d4e11fcad4c7" class="">15. Emotional Energy Budget</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-875d-ed7c0c3be670" class="">Regulation requires metabolic energy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-84e5-f497afcdaef4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
RegCapacity \propto EnergyReserve</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-abb4-c741fc1c8d22" class="">Sleep deprivation ↓ EI.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-9cc0-e84e3fb97181"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-b69b-d25d620eb318" class="">16. Hyperarousal Distorts Perception</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-9afc-e77570fd2eda" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
PerceptualAccuracy \propto \frac{1}{ArousalLevel}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-9aeb-e1fb16e044fe" class="">Too high arousal reduces empathy precision.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-ab6e-f8a6cd208ad0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-94ae-f84847f62c7a" class="">17. Emotional Noise Law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-aad4-e4983455e99c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Noise \uparrow \Rightarrow SignalDetection \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-bd8c-db5f6c952789" class="">Chaotic environments reduce EI expression.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-9ea9-e8dd863de644"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-856e-c831a6189750" class="">18. Boundary Strength Invariant</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-98cd-e4c31c9f7597" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
HealthyBoundaries \propto SelfClarity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-a253-eee939bcc4e1" class="">Weak self-concept → porous boundaries.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-85a9-e8f9946bce61"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-8429-f77e43ccaf35" class="">19. Emotional Damping Coefficient</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-a8d0-cd38a69ce937" class="">Define damping coefficient D:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-a561-f05a507f6e8d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D = \frac{RecoverySpeed}{TriggerIntensity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-b507-e69e2b3aabe9" class="">High D → stable personality.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-8990-ebf4e2305955"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-bc08-f56c4a2b829f" class="">20. Civilization-Level EI</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-84a2-c724652c4d33" class="">At population level:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-acb0-c14862131400" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CollectiveStability \propto Mean(EI) - InformationNoise</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-947c-d052b6255011" class="">High noise environments reduce collective EI.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-8f83-ee8da949d9ac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-8004-e062159a51ba" class="">III. Deep Structural Summary</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-81ac-e7a2f0586ce3" class="">Emotional Intelligence behaves like:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-b433-db82b095acda" class="">A control system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-86ba-e2af4a83d9a8" class="">Input: stimulus</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-8315-e39fae9a2a62" class="">Processing: awareness + modulation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-8154-fd20e1f6332c" class="">Output: calibrated response</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-a314-ca5e69952bc6" class="">Feedback: repair + learning</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-98a1-ffb6619b9749" class="">Mathematically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-a52b-fed0bede5abb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Output = f(Input, Awareness, Regulation, Energy, Integrity)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-9e76-dc2dd0c0d605" class="">System collapses when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-877e-e953e309a958" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
TriggerLoad &gt; RegulationBandwidth</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-aefa-ec2e391f8787"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-bc7e-c79ba84b6abe" class="">IV. Meta-Invariant</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-8269-d571e9cb986d" class="">EI is not softness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-a1f8-e917531111b7" class="">It is <strong>error-correction capacity under stress</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-b20d-ce8ac6c996b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EI = StabilityUnderPerturbation</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-924c-e7dd398b9ddb" class="">The higher the perturbation tolerance without identity collapse, the higher the EI.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-87a7-fad34aa0c965"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-bf29-e94d7582ca71" class="">If you want next layer, I can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-a64c-ec8cb3adb81f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Map EI to autonomic nervous system mathematically</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-a82c-fff822acbea9" class="">• Model high-EI vs low-EI relationship outcomes probabilistically</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-b165-c448ce6ed4d8" class="">• Build a 50-equation advanced EI system model</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-b501-e76a7f67589f" class="">• Map EI across civilizations and power hierarchies</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-a289-e0ec1c5ec42a" class="">Choose depth.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-ad6c-e06e5b2b04fb" class="">Below are <strong>100 more</strong> (101–200). Same format: each item is a <strong>loop or invariant</strong> with an <strong>equation</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-8e7d-d86067417450"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-96bc-d0a2c4125019" class="">H. Control theory, stability, and bounded intervention (101–120)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-89cd-c3c36fa68e42" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>State evolution (discrete)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-9301-e4cb8a83a383" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=F(x_t,u_t,Z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-bc67-e30b6d8008a5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>State evolution (continuous)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-a3a0-c217673b0d87" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
dx_t=f(x_t,u_t)dt+\Sigma(x_t)dW_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-8271-eae3b08d8adb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance basin invariance</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-932a-e612bafb7a40" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t\in\mathcal{D}\ \forall t\in[0,T]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-bc1b-dea9a94c9740" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Jacobian spectral stability</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-8326-fc462570a977" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho\!\left(\frac{\partial F}{\partial x}\right)&lt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-9ff1-c5a19d4db1af" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Lyapunov decrease condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-88f7-f8d69e790e18" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta V = V(x_{t+1})-V(x_t)&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-a1d7-f71918d57a10" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Generator negativity (stochastic stability)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-8b25-e8e9f4989410" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}V(x)=\nabla V\cdot f+\tfrac12\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^T\nabla^2V\Sigma)&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-914a-c0d63a63f048" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Bounded control energy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-a6ec-c5bce33a9411" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_u=\int_0^T \|u(t)\|^2dt\le \bar E_u</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-9047-f9e71d2bb1a1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Emergency action saturation bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-8385-f67ec3a4c187" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|u_{em}(t)\|\le \bar u</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-af62-ca5cecea3694" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Control effort increases entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-9cda-f354acc1fd0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial \mathcal{E}}{\partial E_u}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-934e-fe80f0be166d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Passive stability target</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-98da-f487b6dd3006" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J)\ll 1 \text{ with } u\approx 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-8963-dcd06aa464de" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Recovery time vs spectral gap</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9a7b-cdb99f28779e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{rec}\sim \frac{1}{1-\rho(J)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-b410-f96453810b3e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Shock exit probability bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-adda-f7fc80eb6de7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\tau_{\partial \mathcal{D}}&lt;T)\le \delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-ad8a-c8be0f002b97" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Variance bound under stress</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-a0bd-e40fbd35cd33" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma\Sigma^T)\le \bar\sigma^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9c85-efc6710cd087" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Mean reversion model</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-afe4-fc4c1dbe6461" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
dx_t=-k(x_t-x^\*)dt+\Sigma dW_t,\quad k&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-9c91-ea9ed5c563d6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Control gain instability</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-93d0-fb05be733bef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
k_g\uparrow \Rightarrow |\lambda_i|\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-b600-cdf21aabcc3a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Oscillation condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-acd5-f3d4543153cf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists \lambda:\ \Im(\lambda)\neq 0 \ \wedge\ |\lambda|\approx 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-a9f6-cf85296ac802" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Queue stability (control)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-a643-e1e66e289c16" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lambda_{in}&lt;\mu_{out}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-8df0-db1cab147e7d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Backpressure control invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-8bc7-c0226dc140b7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Backlog\le \bar b \Rightarrow \text{throttle}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-adc6-f8e7e823c11f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Stability–adaptability tradeoff</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-af9e-e0da1a3987f6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta\theta \uparrow \Rightarrow \rho(J)\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-bbad-d7f22d72d583" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Bounded adaptability condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-bd43-f695499b2098" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\Delta\theta\|\le \delta_\theta \ \wedge\ \rho(J)&lt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-80f5-c79ea0dc2228"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-a6b5-de10aa490aa6" class="">I. Information, observability, and auditability (121–140)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-8460-ef9e68b2d143" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Observability condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-9d2e-c7d1bb29f437" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}(\mathcal{O})=|x|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-aa92-ec08195bf5c8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Audit time bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-ae27-d83a23f9125d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{audit}\le \bar T_{audit}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-a668-f0c2b0c44544" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Evidence-binding invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-992a-e7db54411cb9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Claim \Rightarrow \exists Evidence \in Log</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-91b0-ffd254fdc479" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Tamper-evidence invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-9011-c6091658aa98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(record)\in Log \Rightarrow \Pr(\text{undetected tamper})\le 2^{-\kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-9c7a-c5f638ad7695" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Telemetry completeness</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-94d4-f7756da9bf9b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall k\in K_{risk},\ \exists metric(k)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-8795-fd780f1ea2f7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Uncertainty calibration</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-9772-cbed08ab49c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{correct}\mid conf=c)\approx c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-92d9-ef7b94ffdea3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Overconfidence penalty</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-9769-d2a14a532cca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Loss \uparrow \text{ when } conf \gg \Pr(\text{correct})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-a432-c3424ca5b4ca" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Information asymmetry increases arbitrage</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-acfd-c0995c5cfdee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{arb}\uparrow \text{ as } Asymmetry \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-9ed0-ebc553d63834" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Transparency reduces asymmetry</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-a27e-f659e65fba17" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial Asymmetry}{\partial Transparency}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b654-fd4320ea0310" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Disclosure delay increases rumor</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-983e-cf9df54d7420" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dRumor}{dt}=\alpha Opacity-\beta Transparency</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-9f8f-f738c1942182" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Policy description length bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-94ea-d5afb4baf5af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DL(\theta)\le \bar{DL}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-850c-f191c927750f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Policy entropy bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-aa81-ce0162264d45" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(\theta)\le \bar H_\theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9f66-c681230fe12a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Compression–audit tradeoff</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-b053-ef43cd216a75" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DL\downarrow \Rightarrow T_{audit}\downarrow \ \text{if evidence preserved}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-a5a1-ffd0460fac55" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Receipt verifiability</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-9fd5-fc35684b236d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Verify(Receipt)=1 \Rightarrow Valid(Event)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-9af3-e4f6ce106d86" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Replay + proof invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-a8b4-fb3d0745bb7d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Replay \wedge Verify \Rightarrow Deterministic(State)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-b52e-f420825edf21" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>SBOM completeness</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-b796-c74e7bdcd191" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall dep\in Dep,\ dep\in SBOM</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-82e6-d5b166aab1e0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Supply-chain verification</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-85f1-d3a80272924d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Verified(dep)=1 \ \forall dep</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-9de4-c853faa57703" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Log continuity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-8392-d1f631844e61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(Log_t)\ \text{chains to}\ H(Log_{t+1})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-802c-ca35f773e699" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Auditability reduces compliance cost</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-8a07-e676627079b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial R_{comp}}{\partial T_{audit}}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-9219-f8a46961a9df" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Auditability increases adoption</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-99f0-ee73eb635340" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial U}{\partial (1/T_{audit})}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-83ef-e67cf4068c71"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-ae65-d1d1441458b8" class="">J. Mechanism design and incentive compatibility (141–160)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-9b71-d8f1f6e464fd" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Attacker payoff</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-b256-dffdeac9bb52" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{att}=\Pr(success)\cdot Gain - Cost</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-ba48-f58439fe2e9a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Deterrence condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-87fd-c7523abd5763" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
