Proof_{net}=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-ba20-d620cf23e036" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Clearing fund adequacy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-b723-d60f93f22f62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fund \ge VaR_{stress}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-b217-e4e249b20cf3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Rehypothecation increases systemic coupling</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-b0b5-f6be96b3a64e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \uparrow \text{ with reuse ratio } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-b90a-c49e6bddd0d5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reuse ratio cap</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-ac12-f67006f685fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ReuseRatio \le r_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9298-e169718588d7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Market stability requires depth buffer</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-8c2d-fdbc465ea12d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Depth \ge \alpha \cdot \text{largest forced flow}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-b9e2-fb1e66745f37"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-a13a-e8e5fa0115a1" class="">G. Belief dynamics, reflexivity, and phase transition (91–100)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-8c7f-c884a201613e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Belief update (Bayes)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-987d-fa1805f15db1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(H|data)=\frac{P(data|H)P(H)}{P(data)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-9e25-d4b8d432e179" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Confidence proxy affects liquidity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-b9da-f8afe905bcf8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \propto \mathbb{E}[P(H)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-8b5e-cef0baa45498" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Incident reduces posterior</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-8c1f-f77ff5ff9a9b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(H)\downarrow \text{ after incident severity } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-b639-ee1856aba091" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Disclosure delay increases rumor growth</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-9829-ecd9d970ff37" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dRumor}{dt}=\alpha Opacity-\beta Transparency</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-af4e-c8f0de3a8766" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Narrative-to-adoption coupling</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-88d5-f60bdf1d1729" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU}{dt} \propto N(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-9b75-c1f76852683f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reflexive loop gain</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-823a-cfbb1f06cb61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_r=\left(\frac{\partial U}{\partial N}\right)\left(\frac{\partial N}{\partial U}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-a062-f82eaea12d54" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reflexive stability condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-a01f-d2fdc24d2ad4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_r &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-b30b-d157c30e34ac" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Stress parameter</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-80f6-dade9f3da0b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(t)=R(t)\,C(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-a5f7-f61bfc5d6f58" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Phase transition threshold</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-9b68-e73ac5ac7573" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(t)\ge S_c \Rightarrow \text{regime shift}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-a101-c755fa295d52" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance persistence condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-80f6-cf1aaecab6bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists T:\ \forall t\in[0,T],\ EL\le\epsilon,\ MPL_p\le\Lambda,\ P\le P_{max},\ S(t)&lt;S_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-afa7-dc42568726bd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-bf0c-ed40af828268" class="">If you want the next step, pick the target system type (AI model training reliability, cyber defense platform, digital money rail, or governance stack). I will instantiate these 100 into a single concrete state vector , control vector , and measurable telemetry map.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-a3fb-ce6052e3cb77" class="">Enhance AI learning abilities</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-8f24-ce84ed82d494" class="">1) Improve the learning signal</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-ac53-c08cfe0d789f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cleaner targets:</strong> reduce label noise; prefer verified, high-agreement supervision.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b5bf-e7bc972dc51a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Hard-negative mining:</strong> actively sample confusing examples to sharpen boundaries.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-8eb4-dfd5845b60f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Better reward models (for RL):</strong> reward consistency, correctness, and constraint satisfaction; penalize “confident wrong.”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-9a33-cbff534532ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Uncertainty-aware training:</strong> train the model to say “unknown” when evidence is insufficient.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-8e2a-fb6973a5e5d7" class="">2) Increase effective data efficiency</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-bcdc-f406912ac7b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Curriculum learning:</strong> easy → medium → hard; introduce edge cases late.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-9fbe-c195bb8f547d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Active learning loops:</strong> deploy → collect failures → retrain on failures.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-ba54-f18e831b0feb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Self-training with filters:</strong> generate candidates, then keep only those that pass strong validators (tests, solvers, constraints).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-99a8-f35493e49c3b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Data mixing discipline:</strong> separate “style/fluency” from “truth/skills” datasets; control ratios.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-aba1-c0180633dd96" class="">3) Strengthen generalization (not memorization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-8784-ca6370619b19" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Counterfactual augmentation:</strong> vary irrelevant features while keeping the label fixed.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-8c57-e8174ef97826" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invariant-risk training:</strong> enforce stable features across domains/languages/formats.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-90d3-da5aa9f4d2c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Adversarial training:</strong> train against prompt-injection patterns, deceptive inputs, and distribution shift.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-bfe5-c2ef9d55102a" class="">4) Improve reasoning and long-horizon capability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-b46f-d499451287b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Tool-augmented training:</strong> teach the model to call calculators, code interpreters, retrieval, verifiers.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-b262-f31bf36a1b8f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Process supervision where reliable:</strong> reward correct intermediate steps when you can validate them (math, code, proofs).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-9e15-ef5b04a12221" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Search + verification:</strong> sample multiple solution paths, then select via checkers (“generate → test → choose”).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-b23a-ebc421aa641b" class="">5) Add memory that is reliable</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-b205-fdf5472cec4c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>External memory (RAG):</strong> retrieve facts instead of storing everything in weights.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-baa5-db62dde9f6a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Write-then-read traces:</strong> store structured notes (claims, sources, timestamps) and re-use them.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-a67e-eb27272e31d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Memory gating:</strong> only persist information that passes validation and is stable over time.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-b5ec-d2d049dc0279" class="">6) Reduce hallucination structurally</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-93ec-fc7a677a7be5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Evidence binding:</strong> require citations/grounding for factual claims (model must point to retrieved passages).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-a57d-f0a95fefb0f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Calibration training:</strong> match confidence to empirical accuracy (penalize overconfidence).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-964d-dcb9df215553" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Refusal and deferral skills:</strong> train explicit “cannot determine” behavior as a success condition.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-a236-cf45b8016310" class="">7) Harden the evaluation loop (most important in practice)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-9f31-d0856e88ffb6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Task-based evals:</strong> unit tests, theorem checkers, code execution, factual QA with sources.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-8151-f13ea6443703" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Shift tests:</strong> evaluate on new domains, new formats, adversarial prompts, and long-context variants.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9cee-c8c383e41a19" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Regression gates:</strong> new training must not degrade key benchmarks.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-bf98-cee5df5b59af" class="">8) Architecture and optimization upgrades (when you control the stack)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-8bba-df795c59e945" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Long-context + attention improvements:</strong> reduce forgetting; stabilize retrieval from earlier tokens.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-a493-c6df854e3cf4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Mixture-of-experts (MoE):</strong> increase capacity without proportional compute cost (with good routing).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-951b-d90d9759e0b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Better optimizers/schedules:</strong> reduce instability; improve sample efficiency.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-bd66-f0a28d946f86"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-bc8f-e5a5b7c301f7" class="">A minimal “learning enhancement” blueprint</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-9ad0-f9d6d0b6e275" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Define <strong>failure taxonomies</strong> (wrong facts, wrong math, unsafe actions, long-context errors).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-980f-eb31d2b76bd3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Build <strong>validators</strong> for each (tests, solvers, retrieval checks, policy checkers).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-8b19-ee5e69fa6c88" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Train with a loop: <strong>deploy → capture failures → synthesize targeted data → retrain → re-evaluate</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-8f6b-e9b7949a1164" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Gate release on <strong>shift + adversarial</strong> evals, not just average scores.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-8bea-f848d32efb88" class="">If you tell me what “learning ability” means in your context (e.g., reasoning, factual reliability, domain adaptation, long-context, tool-use, or autonomy), I can translate this into a concrete training + eval plan with specific metrics and acceptance thresholds.</p></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
